Two prominent realizations are neural predictors that learn dynamics in continuous vector spaces, and hand-built physics engines that expose explicit state and physical laws. Neural predictors scale from data but leave the form of the dynamics implicit; physics engines are inspectable and editable but difficult to construct at scale. We introduce VisualPatchWorld (VPW), which represents world dynamics as code. VPW first selects a qualitative dynamical form with short active probes, then fits that form's free parameters from recorded state-action traces by minimizing multi-step prediction error. The resulting programs can be rolled forward like a simulator, inspected in source form, and used inside model-predictive control; image-derived scene graphs can supply the live state at replan time. Across comparisons with prior code-based world models, VPW attains 69.0% mean planning success and exceeds the strongest code baseline by 23.5 points. The largest gains arise when choosing the correct qualitative dynamics is essential. Under the same planner, the induced models approach ground-truth engine success on navigation and grasp-rich control; a residual gap remains for contact-rich pushing, and checking a shortlist of promising plans in the engine closes most of that gap. These results establish a practical route toward automatically constructed code world models that are useful for planning. Code is available at https://github.com/HKBU-KnowComp/VisualPatchWorld/.

</details>


### [103] [Where Steering Signals Come From: Activation Source Selection in Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25270)
*Jiaran Ye,Lingxu Ran,Zijun Yao,Chenpeng Wang,Yong Jiang,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li,Liangming Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了激活导向（activation steering）中激活源选择（activation source selection）对导向效果的影响，发现导向成功的关键在于使用"执行边界状态"（模型即将产生目标行为时的隐藏状态），而非简单的目标行为已出现的状态。基于此提出了"尾部减法"（tail subtraction）来净化导向信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活导向方法中，导向信号的来源（源上下文和激活读出策略）通常被视为次要细节，缺乏系统性研究。作者旨在揭示激活源选择对导向效果的重要性，并探索其背后的机理。

Method: 在三种指令微调模型和四类导向任务上，固定下游干预方式，仅改变源激活（包括源上下文类型和读出策略），系统比较导向效果；引入"前/后实现"（pre-/post-realization）区分框架分析结果；提出tail subtraction方法，从边界状态中移除共享的提示和延续语义，获取更纯净的导向信号。

Result: 仅改变源激活即可显著改变导向成功率；有效导向信号并非源自目标行为在源文本中的出现，而是来自执行边界状态；基于答案的源之所以有时有效，是因为其有用成分恰好与执行边界方向对齐；tail subtraction能产生更干净、更稳定的导向信号。

Conclusion: 激活导向的效果取决于模型"即将做什么"的表征，而非"已经出现了什么"的表征，这一发现为理解和改进激活导向方法提供了新的理论视角和实践工具。

Abstract: Activation steering controls language models by adding vectors or features to hidden states at inference time, but the upstream source of these steering signals is often treated as a secondary detail. We study this source choice as activation source selection: the combination of source context and activation readout policy used to collect the hidden states from which a steering signal is built. Holding the downstream intervention fixed, we show across three instruction-tuned models and four steering task families that changing only the source activations substantially changes steering success. We further find that effective steering is not explained simply by whether the desired behavior appears in the source text. Instead, strong signals come from execution-boundary states, where the model is about to produce or continue the target behavior. This pre-/post-realization distinction explains why answer-based sources sometimes work: their useful component aligns with execution-boundary directions rather than target appearance alone. Building on this view, we introduce tail subtraction, which removes shared prompt and continuation semantics from boundary states and yields cleaner, more stable steering signals. Overall, our results suggest that steering depends on representations of what the model is about to do, not merely on what has already appeared.

</details>


### [104] [CoSA: Accelerating Long-Context Inference via Proxy-Kernel Co-Designed Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25291)
*Yufei Xue,Lin Niu,Hong Liu,Siran Liu,Hanyong Shao,Wei Liu,Guanghua Yu,Jianchen Zhu,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoSA提出一种免训练的两阶段稀疏注意力协同设计方法，通过核感知代理(KAP)与有序跳跃核(OSK)的耦合，在更低预算下实现更高的长上下文推理精度，128K上下文下注意力加速达4.93倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于代理的块稀疏注意力方法在预算收紧时，代理会不可避免地遗漏重要块，而核只能机械地应用稀疏掩码，导致模型精度显著下降。核心问题在于代理与核之间缺乏协同设计。

Method: 提出CoSA：第一阶段，核感知代理(KAP)在中等预算下选择块并生成有序掩码，规定KV页在内核循环中的访问顺序；第二阶段，有序跳跃核(OSK)应用该掩码，并利用在线softmax统计信息在收紧的预算下进一步跳过更多块。整个过程无需训练。

Result: 在主流LLM主干和长上下文基准测试中，CoSA以更低预算获得更高精度。在128K上下文长度下，注意力加速达4.93倍，端到端首Token时间(TTFT)降低2.53倍，性能退化可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 代理-核协同设计是解决块稀疏注意力中预算-精度矛盾的有效途径，CoSA通过有序掩码和在线softmax统计实现了高效的长上下文推理加速。

Abstract: The quadratic cost of self-attention makes long-context inference prohibitively expensive, and proxy-based block-sparse attention has become a practical remedy. Existing methods typically rely on a proxy to predict a binary sparse mask and a kernel to consume this mask and perform sparse attention computation. Such an approach is effective under moderate budgets. However, as the budget tightens, the estimated proxy inevitably drops some salient blocks, while the kernel can only apply the sparse mask mechanically, leading to an evident drop in model accuracy. We propose CoSA, a two-stage training-free Sparse Attention under proxy-kernel CO-design, which couples a Kernel-Aware Proxy (KAP) with an Ordered-Skipping Kernel (OSK). In the first stage, the KAP selects blocks under a moderate budget and produces an ordered mask that prescribes the order in which KV pages are visited in the kernel inner loop. In the second stage, the OSK applies this mask and skips more blocks under a tightened budget given online-softmax statistics. Across mainstream LLM backbones and long-context benchmarks, CoSA attains higher accuracy at lower budgets. Impressively, CoSA achieves a 4.93$\times$ attention speedup and reduces end-to-end Time-to-First-Token by 2.53$\times$ under a context length of 128K with negligible performance degradation.

</details>


### [105] [Toward a systematic method for identifying language areas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25305)
*Hiram Ring*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于地理聚类的方法，用于系统性地识别语言区域（包括宏观区域和局部语言联盟），以替代传统依赖专家判断的划分方式，该方法生成的全球宏观区域与既有分组高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言类型学研究中，宏观区域（macroareas）作为控制变量用于解决自相关问题（语言间的接触与谱系继承导致的非独立性），但现有宏观区域的划分主要依赖专家主观判断，且多局限于大陆尺度，缺乏系统性的识别方法。

Method: 提出一种简单的地理聚类方法，可对任意区域进行分组识别，既能生成全球尺度的宏观区域，也能识别局部的语言联盟（sprachbund）。

Result: 该方法生成的全球宏观区域与现有专家划分（如 Hammarström & Donohue 2014; Nichols et al. 2013）大体一致，同时在局部尺度上成功识别出一个著名的语言联盟。

Conclusion: 该地理聚类方法为语言区域的识别提供了一种系统性、可复制的替代方案，有助于更可靠地区分语言的普遍属性与区域特异性属性。

Abstract: Macroareas are geographical areas used in typological research for grouping variables of interest. In linguistic typology, languages in a given macroarea are considered to have potential for contact, in contrast to those outside the area, where contact is less likely. Along with language family membership, macroareas are used as controls for models in linguistic typology, in an attempt to address the problem of autocorrelation - the observation that historical developments or typological patterns may be due to contact between neighboring languages and/or inheritance from a common ancestral language. Macroareas are therefore a central aspect of research that seeks to separate universal properties of language from local (or language-specific) properties. Existing macroareas largely depend on expert determinations of what constitutes a geographical area of potential contact, and to date have mainly aligned with continents or landmasses (Hammarström and Donohue 2014; Nichols, Witzlack-Makarevich, and Bickel 2013). While there are various historical and theoretical reasons for these groupings, there as of yet has been no systematic approach to identifying such areas for a given region. This paper attempts to address such a gap and move beyond macroarea to identification of language areas of relatively arbitrary size, presenting a simple geographical clustering method for identifying groupings over any area. The method produces a set of worldwide macroareas that largely align with existing groupings, as well as local groupings for a well-known sprachbund.

</details>


### [106] [CAST: Game Solvers as Turn-Level Teachers for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25308)
*Yu Wang,Yi-Kai Zhang,Wentao Shi,Ziang Ye,Yuchun Miao,Yueqing Sun,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Lan-Zhe Guo,Han-Jia Ye,Fuli Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CAST 利用游戏求解器的状态值变化生成回合级信用分配信号，将其注入RLVR中，等效于仅需标量值的on-policy蒸馏，在多个长程决策游戏和零样本任务上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练LLM进行长程游戏决策时，RLVR仅依赖稀疏的最终奖励，无法提供回合级别的信用分配——即不清楚哪个具体决策导致了最终成败。现有过程信号要么昂贵（人工标注），要么不准确（启发式方法）。作者观察到求解器的状态值变化能够揭示行动是否推进了成功进程，因此希望利用这一廉价且准确的信号来改善信用分配。

Method: 提出CAST方法：(1) 使用游戏求解器（如A*搜索）估计每个状态的值函数；(2) 将状态值的变化转化为"solver advantage"，即行动前后状态值之差；(3) 将该优势作为回合级信号注入RLVR训练中；(4) 理论证明，在soft-optimal求解器假设下，最大化solver advantage等价于对求解器进行on-policy蒸馏，且仅需标量值而非教师模型的完整logits。

Result: 在Sokoban、Minesweeper、Rush Hour三款游戏上，CAST在域内和未见难度评估下均超越所有训练基线；在ALFWorld和WebShop的零样本迁移中取得最高平均性能。

Conclusion: CAST通过从求解器中提取廉价而准确的回合级信用信号，有效弥补了RLVR稀疏奖励的不足。其理论等价于on-policy蒸馏的特性使其既简单又高效，为LLM在长程决策任务中的训练提供了新方向。

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) to act in long-horizon games is a promising step toward generalist decision-making, yet reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) relies on sparse final rewards that reveal little about which decisions determine success. Denser process signals could supply this missing turn-level credit, but existing sources are hard to keep both cheap and accurate. We observe that changes in a game solver's state value reveal whether an action advances the state toward success. Building on this insight, we propose CAST (Credit Assignment from Solver Teachers), which converts these value changes into solver advantages and injects them into RLVR as turn-level signals. We further show that, under a soft-optimal solver assumption, maximizing the solver advantage is equivalent to on-policy distillation from the solver, requiring only scalar values rather than teacher logits. Across Sokoban, Minesweeper, and Rush Hour, CAST outperforms all trained baselines on every game under both in-domain and unseen-difficulty evaluation and achieves the highest average zero-shot performance on ALFWorld and WebShop. Our code is available at https://github.com/Wloner0809/CAST.

</details>


### [107] [Every Time I Hire a Linguist, Inference Costs Go Down: On Linguistic Rules as Effective Prompt Compressors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25335)
*Jianfei Ma,Zhaoxin Feng,Emmanuele Chersoni,Si Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需LM前向传播的提示压缩方法，通过演化搜索在词汇、句法、语义和篇章层面寻找确定性语言规则组合，在轻中度压缩下性能可媲美现有先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示压缩方法依赖语言模型前向传播来评分token重要性，计算成本高。作者质疑这种精细且昂贵的token选择是否必要，转而探索能否仅用确定性语言规则实现有效压缩。

Method: 在词汇、句法、语义、篇章四个层面定义语言规则种子，通过离线演化搜索（evolutionary search）寻找最优规则组合。压缩过程仅需CPU处理，无需LM前向传播。评估采用双路径协议（Direct路径和Reconstruction路径）以平衡压缩质量与重建保真度。

Result: 在短文本、多文档推理和对话记忆QA数据集上，演化出的压缩器在轻中度压缩下性能与近期先进提示压缩策略相当；压缩强度增大时性能下降；Direct路径和Reconstruction路径呈现不同模式；有效压缩融合了多语言层面的信号，且随压缩率提高，规则从token剪枝转向句子提取。

Conclusion: 仅依赖语言规则即可实现有竞争力的提示压缩，表明LM-based token评分并非必需，为低成本、高效的提示压缩提供了新方向。

Abstract: Prompt compression shortens LLM input to reduce inference cost, yet existing methods score token importance through LM forward passes. It remains questionable whether such nuanced, costly token selection is necessary. Compression requires identifying informative content, a problem that linguistic research has long addressed through cues that can be operationalized as deterministic rules. We therefore ask: can \textbf{linguistic rules alone} serve as effective prompt compressors, without LM-based scoring at compression time?
  To address this, we conduct offline evolutionary search over lexical, syntactic, semantic, and discourse seeds to find competitive rule combinations. The resulting linguistic compressor requires no LM forward pass at deployment and uses only CPU-side processing for compression. We evaluate it with a dual-path protocol to balance compression quality and reconstruction fidelity.
  Across short passages, multi-document reasoning, and dialogue-memory QA datasets, evolved compressors achieve performance similar to that of recent advanced prompt-compression strategies. Performance is strongest under light-to-moderate compression and degrades as compression becomes more aggressive, while the Direct and Reconstruction paths exhibit distinct patterns. Evolutionary analysis reveals that effective compression fuses signals across linguistic levels and, as the compression ratio increases, rules shift from token pruning to sentence extraction.

</details>


### [108] [Temporal-Distance JEPA: Plan-Aware Representation Learning for Latent World Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25337)
*Jiaxin Bai,Jiaxuan Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TD-JEPA 提出在 JEPA 世界模型框架中，从离线无奖励轨迹中挖掘有向时序进度代价，以此替代依赖嵌入几何的隐式代价，用于潜在模型预测控制中的多步规划。该方法在多个环境中匹配或超越 LeWM 和 RC-aux 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 JEPA 规划器通常将规划排序任务委托给嵌入空间的几何属性（如潜在欧氏距离），而这种几何结构是表征学习的副产品，并非专门从离线日志中挖掘的进度代价。这导致了训练（短时程潜在预测）与规划（基于目标进度的多步排序）之间存在"训练-规划鸿沟"。

Method: 提出 TD-JEPA，保留 JEPA 的编码器-预测器骨干，额外从无奖励轨迹中挖掘有向时序代价：使用同轨迹步序提供正样本目标，跨轨迹对作为启发式负样本，并引入 rollout 一致性项匹配规划时域。挖掘出的监督信号既可在拓扑进度主导时作为部署规划代价，也可在接触几何主导时作为表征信号改善欧氏规划。

Result: 在锁定评估下，部署挖掘代价将 Two-Room 成功率从 LeWM 的 97.4% 提升至 100.0%；同一时间训练检查点上共享欧氏规划使 OGB-Cube 超越 LeWM 14.2 分，并改善了 Push-T。在全部环境中均匹配或超越 LeWM 与 RC-aux 基线。消融实验证实有向头、跨轨迹负样本和 rollout 一致性各自均有贡献。

Conclusion: TD-JEPA 通过在离线日志中发现时序进度结构，并将代价形式与规划时部署协同设计，有效缩小了 JEPA 世界模型规划器的训练-规划鸿沟。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) learn world models by predicting in representation space rather than reconstructing pixels, making them a natural backbone for latent model predictive control from offline demonstration logs. JEPA-style training optimizes short-horizon latent prediction, whereas planning requires a multi-step ranking of imagined futures by goal progress. Prior JEPA planners often inherit that ranking from embedding geometry, typically latent Euclidean distance, which arises as a byproduct of representation learning rather than as a progress cost mined from the logs. We propose temporal-distance JEPA (TD-JEPA), which retains the LeWM encoder--predictor backbone and mines a directed temporal cost from reward-free trajectories: same-trajectory step order supplies positive targets, cross-trajectory pairs act as heuristic negatives, and a rollout-consistency term matches the planner horizon. The mined supervision serves two roles: as the deployed planning cost when progress is topological, and as a representation signal that improves Euclidean planning when contact geometry dominates. Under locked evaluation, deploying the mined cost raises Two-Room success to 100.0% versus LeWM's 97.4%, while shared Euclidean planning on the same temporally trained checkpoint raises OGB-Cube by 14.2 points over LeWM and improves Push-T. Against LeWM and the concurrent RC-aux baseline under locked evaluation, TD-JEPA matches or exceeds both methods on every environment. Ablations show that the directed head, cross-trajectory negatives, and rollout consistency each contribute. TD-JEPA narrows the train--plan gap for JEPA world-model planners by discovering temporal progress structure in offline logs and co-designing cost form with plan-time deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/HKBU-KnowComp/TD-JEPA.

</details>


### [109] [Inspect India Evals: An Open Benchmarking Framework for Evaluating Large Language Models in the Indian Linguistic and Cultural Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25375)
*Abhishek Kumar Singh,Shrey Nag,Sachita,Lipi Goel,Rajeshwar Singh Janwar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对印度多语言、多文化场景缺乏本土化LLM评估基准的问题，作者构建了Inspect India Evals开源框架，包含六项本土化基准测试，并对五款开源模型进行了评估，揭示了模型在印度语境下的安全性、公平性和文化知识方面的表现差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流LLM基准（MMLU、BIG-Bench、TruthfulQA）几乎完全以英语和西方为中心，无法识别印度独特的文化、语言、安全与公平性失效模式。印度拥有14亿人口、22种官方语言及数百种地方文化，亟需本土化评估框架来填补这一空白。

Method: 基于英国AISI的Inspect AI平台构建开源框架Inspect India Evals，包含六项基准：①覆盖16种印度语言的多语言MMLU；②改编自BBQ的印度社会偏见测试BharatBBQ；③数字公共基础设施（DPI）安全评估；④使用印度语言有害提示的多语言安全测试；⑤多轮越狱抵抗测试；⑥基于LLM-as-judge评分的印度文化知识基准。使用五款8B至32B参数的开源权重模型进行测试。

Result: Sarvam-M 24B和Gemma 2 27B表现最优，在综合印度公平指数上均达80%；Sarvam-M在印度文化知识和DPI安全合规方面甚至超越更大的32B模型；所有模型在多语言安全测试中均达到100%拒绝率；DPI安全合规率从20%到100%不等，差异显著。

Conclusion: Inspect India Evals有效填补了印度本土化LLM评估的空白，揭示了模型在印度语境下安全性与公平性的显著差异。该开源框架支持与UK AISI注册中心集成，任何人都可复现或扩展此项工作。

Abstract: India is a vast nation of over 1.4 billion people, varied by hundreds of diverse and locally specific traditions and cultures and 22 officially recognized languages. Large language models (LLMs) are now being deployed on a massive scale throughout the mainland as well as in remote villages. However, the common benchmarks - MMLU, BIG-Bench, and TruthfulQA are almost exclusively English- and Western-centric. They do not identify those safety, fairness, and accuracy failures unique to the Indian context. That is the gap Inspect India Evals seeks to fill. It is an open-source framework built on top of UK AISI's Inspect AI platform. It has six benchmarks: Multilingual MMLU across sixteen Indian languages, BharatBBQ (our adaptation of BBQ for Indian social bias), a safety evaluation for Digital Public Infrastructure, a multilingual safety test using harmful prompts in Indian languages, a multi-turn jailbreak resistance test, and an Indian cultural knowledge benchmark scored using LLM-as-judge rubrics. In this study, we tested five open-weight models ranging from 8B to 32B parameters. Sarvam-M 24B and Gemma 2 27B came out on top, both scoring 80% on the composite India Fairness Index, with Sarvam-M even beating larger 32B models on Indian cultural knowledge and DPI safety compliance. All models scored 100% refusal on Multilingual Safety, whereas DPI safety varied from 20% to 100%. The framework is public. It's built to work with the UK AISI registry. Anyone can reproduce or extend this work.

</details>


### [110] [Memory for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25380)
*Sining Zhoubian,Dan Zhang,Evgeny Kharlamov,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以架构为中心的大语言模型记忆机制分类法，沿三个正交轴（表征形式、更新动态、持久性）系统化梳理了LLM中记忆技术的碎片化研究格局，并深入分析了混合架构、效率权衡与评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型中记忆机制的研究快速演进，涌现出瞬时注意力、循环状态动态、参数高效适配、可扩展查找存储等多种策略，但当前研究格局高度碎片化，缺乏统一的概念框架来界定不同记忆范式之间的边界与关联。

Method: 本文提出了一套系统的、以架构为中心的记忆分类学框架，沿三个正交轴进行分类：(1) 表征形式——隐式记忆 vs. 显式记忆；(2) 更新动态——离线更新 vs. 在线更新；(3) 持久性——短期记忆 vs. 长期记忆。在此基础上，进一步形式化了支配记忆写入、路由、状态转换和巩固的细粒度机制。

Result: 该统一框架有效阐明了"与计算耦合的记忆"和"可独立寻址的记忆"之间的概念边界，成功桥接了不同的架构范式。同时，论文对混合记忆架构、系统级效率权衡以及多维度评估方法论进行了批判性分析。

Conclusion: 本综述通过将分散的研究进展整合为统一框架，描绘了以记忆为中心的大语言模型设计的发展轨迹，为未来可扩展与自适应语言建模的创新提供了原则性基础。

Abstract: Memory has evolved into a foundational architectural dimension in large language models (LLMs), shifting from an implicit byproduct of computation to a spectrum of explicit, controllable mechanisms. While recent advances introduce diverse strategies---spanning transient attention, recurrent state dynamics, parameter-efficient adaptations, and scalable lookup storage---this rapid evolution has led to a highly fragmented research landscape. In this survey, we present a systematic, architecture-centric taxonomy of memory in LLMs. Our framework characterizes memory along three orthogonal axes: representation (implicit versus explicit), update dynamics (offline versus online), and persistence (short-term versus long-term). We further formalize the granular mechanisms dictating memory writing, routing, state transitions, and consolidation. This unified perspective elucidates the conceptual boundaries between computation-coupled and independently addressable memory, effectively bridging disparate architectural paradigms. Additionally, we critically analyze hybrid memory architectures, system-level efficiency trade-offs, and multi-dimensional evaluation methodologies. By consolidating these scattered advancements into a cohesive framework, this survey charts the trajectory of memory-centric LLM design and provides a principled foundation for future innovations in scalable and adaptive language modeling.

</details>


### [111] [Phase Structure in Rotary Attention: A Spectral Framework for Semantic Continuity and Execution-Boundary Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25507)
*Abraham Chachamovits*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文为分析Transformer语言模型中的旋转位置编码（RoPE）开发了一个有界谱框架，将注意力分数推导为幅度加权余弦项之和，证明了局部稳定性引理，并引入复模态坐标与加权相干泛函，以严格区分表征连续性与执行边界可采性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多通过向量几何分析Transformer，但有序上下文和旋转位置编码在查询-键交互中引入了显式的相位结构。作者旨在建立一个谱框架，在不将语言模型视为物理波系统的情况下，系统考察旋转相位对齐、隐藏状态连续性和语义漂移。

Method: （1）将有序隐藏状态序列（而非词汇索引）确定为谱分解的有效域；（2）将RoPE注意力分数推导为幅度加权余弦项之和，并证明局部稳定性引理——有界相位位移限制预softmax分数的退化；（3）在固定正交方向对上定义复模态坐标，超越原生RoPE坐标进行相位分析；（4）为隐藏状态轨迹引入加权相干泛函；（5）基于上述构造，严格区分表征连续性与执行边界可采性，并将该框架与几何、谱、相位调制、表征分析及机制可解释性等现有范式进行定位比较。

Result: （1）局部稳定性引理：一致有界的相位位移限制预softmax注意力分数的退化；（2）复模态坐标使相位分析可扩展至非原生RoPE坐标；（3）加权相干泛函能够量化隐藏状态轨迹的相干性；（4）在表征连续性与执行边界可采性之间建立了严格的概念区分：内部相干性可描述任务相关关系的保持，但不能授权后果性转换。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一个理论与方法论方案，用于判定何时谱结构能够解释连续性，以及何时治理必须作为执行之上的外部谓词。该工作推进了对RoPE相位结构的理解，并为语言模型中的连续性分析建立了更严谨的边界条件。

Abstract: Transformer language models are usually analyzed through vector geometry, yet ordered context and rotary position encoding introduce explicit phase structure into query-key interactions. This paper develops a bounded spectral framework for examining rotary phase alignment, hidden-state continuity, and semantic drift without treating language models as literal physical wave systems. It first identifies ordered hidden-state sequences, rather than vocabulary indices, as valid domains for spectral decomposition. It then derives the Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) attention score as a sum of magnitude-weighted cosine terms and proves a local stability lemma: uniformly bounded phase displacement limits degradation of the corresponding pre-softmax score. To extend phase analysis beyond native RoPE coordinates, the paper defines complex modal coordinates over fixed orthonormal direction pairs and introduces a weighted coherence functional for hidden-state trajectories. These constructions support a strict distinction between representational continuity and execution-boundary admissibility. Internal coherence may describe preservation of task-relevant relations, but it cannot authorize a consequential transition. Positioned against existing geometric, spectral, phase-modulation, representation-analysis, and mechanistic-interpretability accounts, the framework contributes a theoretical and methodological program for determining when spectral structure explains continuity and when governance must remain an external predicate over execution.

</details>


### [112] [IRIS: Reusable Identity Representations from Frozen LLMs for Entity Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25579)
*Xinran Liu,Shengtao Li,Shouqian Shi,Ge Wang,Xin-Wei Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IRIS是一种无需训练的实体对齐框架，通过从冻结的大语言模型内部状态中提取"虹膜式"身份签名，为每个实体构建一个稳定、可跨知识图谱直接比较的表示空间，在多个基准上取得了近乎完美的Hits@1性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统EA方法依赖显式图结构和文本字段，语义理解不足，难以在异构描述下识别同一实体并区分语义相似的实体。现有基于LLM的方法虽语义理解更强，但仅将这种能力用于辅助生成或候选条件决策，未能将语义理解蒸馏到一个稳定、可直接比较的身份空间中，导致对齐依赖特定的KG对或候选集，匹配上下文变化时需重复处理。

Method: IRIS是一种无需训练的框架。其核心思想是为每个实体构建一个"虹膜式签名"（iris-like signature），编码其独特且稳定的身份特征。具体做法是：通过从冻结的LLM中引出面向身份的上下文表示（identity-oriented contextual representations），形成一个共享空间。在该空间中，每个实体只需编码一次，即可通过直接相似度比较在不同KG之间完成对齐，无需依赖配对表示构建或逐候选的LLM推理。

Result: 在四个已建立的EA基准数据集（D-Y-15K V2、DBP-WIKI、ICEWS-WIKI、ICEWS-YAGO）和两个冻结LLM骨干网络上，IRIS的最佳变体分别达到了100.00、99.38、98.31和97.99的Hits@1分数。

Conclusion: IRIS成功将LLM的深层语义理解能力转化为稳定、可复用、可直接比较的实体身份表示，克服了现有LLM-based EA方法依赖特定匹配上下文的局限性，实现了高效且高精度的实体对齐。

Abstract: Entity alignment (EA) identifies entities across knowledge graphs (KGs) that refer to the same real-world object. Conventional EA methods mainly exploit explicit graph structures and textual fields, which often provide insufficient semantic understanding to recognize the same entity under heterogeneous descriptions and distinguish it from semantically similar entities. Although large language models (LLMs) offer deeper entity understanding, existing LLM-based EA methods largely use this capability for auxiliary generation or candidate-conditioned decisions. Consequently, such understanding is not distilled into a stable and directly comparable identity space, leaving alignment tied to specific KG pairs or candidate sets and requiring repeated processing as the matching context changes. To address these limitations, we propose IRIS (Identity Representations from Internal States), a training-free framework that constructs for each entity an iris-like signature encoding its distinctive and stable identity characteristics. IRIS derives these signatures by eliciting identity-oriented contextual representations from a frozen LLM, thereby forming a shared space in which each entity is encoded once and can be aligned across different KGs through direct similarity comparison, without pair-dependent representation construction or candidate-wise LLM inference. Across four established EA benchmarks and two frozen LLM backbones, the best IRIS variants achieve Hits@1 scores of 100.00, 99.38, 98.31, and 97.99 on D-Y-15K V2, DBP-WIKI, ICEWS-WIKI, and ICEWS-YAGO, respectively.

</details>


### [113] [Evaluation of forced alignment of code-mixed speech: the case of Hindi-English](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25581)
*Ayushi Pandey,Pamir Gogoi,Kevin Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了使用Montreal Forced Aligner对印地语-英语语码混合语音进行强制对齐的效果，发现基于语码混合数据训练的声学模型和精心设计的词典可以显著提升对齐精度，平均误差低至4.15ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语码混合语音在强制对齐中面临独特挑战，包括扩展的音素库存、拼写错误和说话人变异。现有单语对齐方案在双语混合场景下表现不佳，需要探索有效的对齐策略。

Method: 使用Montreal Forced Aligner进行强制对齐，针对两个核心问题展开：(1) 母语与非母语音素对的自由变异处理；(2) 句中英语词的音素边界检测。采用引导策略（bootstrapping）优化词典设计，并对比了基于句级语码混合数据、单语印地语和孤立英语训练的声学模型性能。

Result: 引导策略构建的词典显著优于未修改的词典。基于句级语码混合数据训练的声学模型平均误差为4.15ms，比单语印地语模型（38.18ms）和孤立英语模型（37.58ms）低约十倍。

Conclusion: 精心设计的词典与语码混合训练数据两者对于双语语音的可靠对齐缺一不可，为语码混合语音的强制对齐提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Code-mixed speech poses unique challenges to forced alignment: expanded inventories, orthographic errors, and speaker variation. We evaluate forced alignment of Hindi-English code-mixed speech using the Montreal Forced Aligner. We address 2 problems: (1) free variation involving native vs non-native pairs and (2) phonemic boundary detection for mid-utterance English words. Bootstrapping strategies substantially outperform unmodified lexicons. Acoustic models trained on sentence-level code-mixed data achieve a mean error of 4.15ms, ie. ten times lower than monolingual Hindi (38.18ms) or isolated English (37.58ms) alternatives. Principled lexicon design and code-mixed training data are both essential for reliable alignment of bilingual speech.

</details>


### [114] [PILA: Plug-and-Play Insertion for LLM-native Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25590)
*Zhaowei Zhang,Yuhan Fu,Yihang Zhang,Xiaohan Liu,Ceyao Zhang,Xiaoyuan Zhang,Yipeng Kang,Tonghan Wang,Yaodong Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PILA，将大语言模型原生广告解耦为独立的条件响应重写模块（sidecar），实现模型无关、可无缝集成、且能在自然度与广告曝光间可控权衡的广告插入方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM原生广告方案将广告与内容生成耦合在单一模型内，与现代API-only或工作流式LLM应用不兼容，且会损害原始响应质量。需要一种解耦、轻量、模型无关的广告插入方案。

Method: 将广告插入重新定义为条件响应重写问题，设计PILA作为轻量级sidecar模块，与上游LLM服务解耦，无需修改基座模型或其工作流即可集成；同时提供用户侧自然度与广告侧曝光度的可控权衡接口。

Result: 在多种上游模型上的实验表明，PILA能持续提升广告效果，同时保持响应质量。

Conclusion: PILA作为一种实用的大语言模型原生广告解决方案具有良好前景，兼顾广告有效性与内容质量。

Abstract: How to monetize large language models (LLMs) by naturally integrating sponsored content into their responses, known as LLM-native advertising, has recently emerged as a critical problem. However, existing solutions entangle advertising with content generation inside a single model, which is incompatible with modern API-only or workflow-based LLM applications and inevitably compromises the original response quality. To address this, we propose PILA, which reformulates ad insertion as a conditional response rewriting problem and decouples it from the upstream service as a lightweight sidecar module. PILA is model-agnostic and can be seamlessly integrated with existing LLM services without modifying the base model or its workflow. It also exposes a controllable trade-off between user-side naturalness and ad-side exposure, offering a practical interface for downstream pricing and deployment. Experiments across diverse upstream models show that \pila consistently improves ad effectiveness while preserving response quality, highlighting its promise as a practical solution for LLM-native advertising.

</details>


### [115] [A Human-in-the-Loop Corpus for LLM-Based Simplification of Scientific Summaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25630)
*Kyuri Im,Michael Färber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究基于LLM的科学文本简化，提出一个人机协作流程，将专家摘要转化为非专业人士可理解的版本。通过两阶段实验（STEM读者评估+CS专家编辑），收集了人类判断与编辑语料，发现GPT简化的文本在可理解性和简洁性上受青睐，但同时强调了保留领域术语和科学论断力度的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨学科研究加速发展，但科学论文在非本领域读者中仍然难以理解。现有科学文本简化工作缺乏高质量的人类反馈语料和系统的简化流程研究，需要探索LLM在跨学科科学传播中的简化能力，并构建可用于训练和基准测试的数据资源。

Method: 以SciSummNet为源语料，首先用GPT-4o-mini生成基线简化文本。第一阶段，由计算机科学以外的STEM领域读者识别困难句子和短语，并对比原文与GPT简化摘要在可理解性、自然性和简洁性三个维度上的表现。第二阶段，由计算机科学专家基于第一阶段反馈进行专家级编辑，产出参考简化文本。最终发布包含人类判断和自动评估结果的语料库。

Result: 第一阶段的人类判断显示，读者在可理解性和简洁性上明确偏好GPT生成的摘要。第二阶段对专家编辑的定性分析表明，保留领域特定术语和维持科学论断的力度（不弱化结论）是高质量简化的关键要素。

Conclusion: 本文构建的人机协同简化工作流有效生成了高质量的跨学科科学交流资源，所发布的语料库可支持科学文本简化系统的训练与基准测试。研究揭示了自动化简化与人工编辑各自的优势与局限，为未来科学传播的LLM应用提供了实践框架和数据基础。

Abstract: Interdisciplinary research is accelerating, yet scientific papers remain difficult to understand outside their home fields. We study large language model (LLM)-based simplification of scientific texts and present a human-in-the-loop workflow that transforms expert summaries into more accessible versions for non-specialists. Using SciSummNet as the source corpus, we first generate baseline simplifications with GPT-4o-mini. In Phase 1, readers from STEM fields outside computer science identify difficult sentences and phrases and compare the original and GPT-simplified summaries in terms of comprehensibility, naturalness, and simplicity. In Phase 2, computer science experts use this feedback to create expert-edited reference simplifications. We release the resulting corpus together with human judgments and automatic evaluation results. The Phase 1 judgments show a clear preference for the GPT-generated summaries in terms of comprehensibility and simplicity, while qualitative analysis of the Phase 2 edits highlights the importance of preserving domain-specific terminology and the strength of scientific claims. The resulting resource supports the training and benchmarking of simplification systems for cross-disciplinary scientific communication.

</details>


### [116] [Construction-Driven Injection: Linguistically-Grounded Edit-Based Code-Mixing Fingerprints for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25633)
*Yongyi Cui,Yue Li,Tianbao Jiang,Xin Yi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LCF（Language-Controlled Fingerprinting）统一框架，联合优化指纹构建与注入两阶段：构建阶段采用代码混合（code-mixing）指纹，利用低资源语言、语义密度替换和语法偏向混合，生成低困惑度且避免误激活的触发器；注入阶段通过高资源多语言表示导出的零空间投影保留知识，并辅以跨语言对齐将权重更新引导至指纹语言的表示子空间，最终实现隐蔽、可检测、无害的持久所有权验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM指纹水印方法将指纹构建与注入两个阶段解耦，存在两大局限：(1)自然语言指纹易被意外激活（accidental activation），乱码指纹易被基于困惑度的检测过滤；(2)解耦设计使注入阶段无法感知触发器的语言结构，错失针对性优化的机会。作者主张指纹构建应驱动注入过程。

Method: LCF统一框架分两步：(1) LCF构建阶段：采用代码混合指纹，结合低资源语言、语义密度替换规则和语法偏向混合策略，生成困惑度远低于乱码基线且避免自然语言误激活的触发器；(2) LCFEdit注入阶段：利用高资源多语言表示导出的零空间投影进行指纹注入以保留模型知识，并通过跨语言对齐步骤将权重更新引导至指纹语言的表示子空间，实现"构建感知"的注入，使更新更具语言信息支撑和稳定性。

Result: 在隐蔽性（imperceptibility）、可检测性（detectability）和无害性（harmlessness）三个维度上的广泛评估表明，该方法实现了持久的所有权验证，同时对模型实用性影响可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 通过构建驱动的统一指纹框架，LCF有效解决了现有方法中指纹易被过滤或误激活的问题，以及注入阶段缺乏语言结构感知的缺陷，为LLM知识产权保护提供了一种更为鲁棒和实用的黑盒可验证所有权方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are costly intellectual assets that remain exposed to unauthorized redistribution and commercial misuse. Injected fingerprints, i.e., trigger--target pairs embedded in model behavior, offer a practical, black-box-verifiable ownership signal, but existing methods decouple the two stages of the fingerprint life cycle: how a fingerprint is constructed and how it is injected. Existing fingerprinting frameworks suffer from two limitations. Natural-language fingerprints are prone to accidental activation, and garbled fingerprints are easily filtered by perplexity-based detection. Furthermore, decoupling construction from injection leaves the latter unaware of the trigger's linguistic structure, missing the opportunity for targeted optimization. We argue that fingerprint construction should drive injection, and present a unified fingerprinting framework that jointly optimizes both stages. First, LCF constructs code-mixing fingerprints by combining low-resource languages under a semantic-density substitution rule and grammar-biased mixing, yielding triggers whose perplexity sits far below garbled baselines while avoiding the accidental-activation failures of natural-language triggers. Second, LCFEdit injects each fingerprint with a null-space projection derived from high-resource multilingual representations that preserves knowledge, augmented by a cross-lingual alignment step that steers the weight update toward the fingerprint language's representation subspace. This construction-aware injection ensures that the update is linguistically informed and therefore more stable. Extensive evaluations on imperceptibility, detectability, and harmlessness demonstrate persistent ownership verification with negligible impact on utility.

</details>


### [117] [MyMentorLLM: A psychotherapy GenAI environment with multimodal voice/text patients, trainees and experts for deliberate practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25667)
*Rodolfo Rizzi,Alessandro Grecucci,Massimo Stella*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MyMentorLLM，一个面向认知行为疗法（CBT）刻意练习的多模态语音与文本模拟环境，生成了2100个完整的CBT训练会话。研究发现模拟患者表达了符合诊断的情绪特征，学员治疗师表现出与真实咨询相似的情绪镜像现象；不同LLM的督导质量差异显著，多数模型高估学员能力，而原生语音到语音模型评分最接近人类。督导反馈在5/7的LLM中改善了模拟治疗师的诊断准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理治疗师需要反复训练和专家督导，但可扩展性存在瓶颈。刻意练习（deliberate practice）是提升治疗技能的有效方法，然而大规模、标准化的模拟训练环境缺失，亟需可扩展的解决方案。

Method: 构建MyMentorLLM多模态模拟环境，基于DSM-5-TR诊断标准设计三类障碍（重度抑郁、广泛性焦虑、边缘型人格障碍）的模拟患者，由受训治疗师与专家督导LLM进行交互。生成2100个完整CBT会话，分析情绪动力学、治疗胜任力与诊断准确性三个维度。

Result: 模拟患者展现出符合障碍特征的情绪轮廓，受训治疗师出现与真实咨询一致的情绪镜像现象；多数LLM督导高估学员胜任力，但原生语音到语音模型的评分最接近人类评分；7个LLM中有5个通过督导反馈提升了模拟治疗师的诊断准确性，且症状识别准确率随模型规模增大而提高。

Conclusion: 该研究证明CBT刻意练习的模拟是可行的，但患者保真度、督导校准以及有害反馈等问题仍需综合评估，方可安全部署于实际培训场景。

Abstract: Psychotherapists need repeated training and supervision by experts; however, scalability is problematic. Here we present MyMentorLLM, a multimodal voice- and text-based simulation environment for deliberate practice, used to generate 2,100 complete Cognitive Behavioural Therapy (CBT) training sessions. Each session links a DSM-5-TR-grounded patient (with major depressive, generalised anxiety or borderline personality disorder), a therapist-in-training and an expert supervisor. As an initial implementation, we adopted CBT because its structured procedures and competency-based supervision facilitate standardised simulation and evaluation. Sessions were analysed for emotional dynamics, therapeutic competence and diagnostic accuracy. Simulated patients expressed disorder-congruent emotional profiles, which trainee therapists mirrored as in real human counselling. The quality of supervision differed across LLMs: while most models overestimated trainees' competences, native speech-to-speech was closest to human scores. Supervisors' feedback led to better diagnoses in simulated psychotherapists in 5 out of 7 LLMs, and symptom identification accuracy increased with model size. This work shows that simulation of deliberate practice is possible for CBT training, although patient fidelity, calibration of supervisors, and harmful feedback should be evaluated together.

</details>


### [118] [SpeechLLM Meets Federated Learning for End-to-End ASR: English and Italian Case Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25716)
*Mohamed Nabih Ali,Daniele Falavigna,Alessio Brutti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了基于语音大语言模型（SpeechLLM）的端到端ASR联邦训练，提出了通信高效的联邦优化策略，在英语和意大利语单语ASR任务上验证了其有效性和稳定性，并通过消融实验分析了不同语音编码器架构的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在ASR系统中已有应用，但在大规模语音语言模型（SpeechLLMs）上的联邦训练研究仍属空白。SpeechLLM具有高维参数空间、梯度通信开销大、分布式计算受限等独特挑战，急需针对性的联邦优化策略。

Method: 设计了一种通信高效的联邦优化策略，专门针对SpeechLLM架构特点：1）应对高维参数空间的优化方法；2）降低梯度通信开销的技术；3）适应分布式计算约束的训练机制。在英语和意大利语单语ASR任务上进行广泛实证评估，并与集中式训练基线对比；同时通过消融实验分析不同语音编码器架构对联邦框架内ASR性能的影响。

Result: 1）所提联邦方法在多种声学条件和说话风格下，取得了与集中式训练基线相当的竞争力；2）实现了具有竞争力的词错误率（WER），同时显著降低了通信成本；3）消融实验揭示了不同语音编码器架构在联邦框架下的性能差异，为分布式训练中的最优模型配置提供了指导。

Conclusion: 本研究为联邦SpeechLLM在实际多语言场景中的部署奠定了实用基础，证明通信高效的联邦训练策略能够在保持ASR性能的同时有效降低通信开销，为未来大规模分布式语音语言模型的隐私保护训练开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables privacy-preserving training of automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems across distributed data sources, yet its application to large-scale speech language models (SpeechLLMs) remains unexplored. This paper presents the first systematic study of federated training for SpeechLLM-based end-to-end ASR systems. We design a communication-efficient federated optimization strategy tailored to the unique challenges of SpeechLLM architectures, addressing high-dimensional parameter spaces, gradient communication overhead, and computational constraints in distributed settings. Through extensive empirical evaluation on monolingual ASR tasks in English and Italian, we demonstrate the effectiveness and stability of our federated approach compared to centralized training baselines across diverse acoustic conditions and speaking styles. Additionally, we conduct a comprehensive ablation study analyzing the impact of different speech encoder architectures on monolingual English ASR performance within the federated framework, providing insights into optimal model configurations for decentralized training. Our results achieve competitive word error rates while reducing communication costs, establishing practical foundations for federated SpeechLLM deployment in real-world multilingual scenarios.

</details>


### [119] [WorkSurface-Bench: Benchmarking Enterprise Agents on Multi-Surface Knowledge Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25765)
*Hao Liang,Meiyi Qiang,Sizhe Qiu,Linzhuang Sun,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了WorkSurface-Bench基准测试，用于评估企业智能体在文档、表格、依赖图等异构知识源之间的"表面路由"能力。包含1,151个原子任务，实验表明正确的表面选择是任务完成的必要但不充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业智能体需要整合文档、表格、依赖图等异构知识源，但现有基准测试通常评估检索或工具使用，未区分智能体是否正确选择了适当的知识源（即表面路由能力）。

Method: 从Workspace-Bench-Lite工作区构建WorkSurface-Bench，包含1,151个原子任务，涵盖文档、表格、图和跨表面问题。参考答案可审计：表格答案通过DuckDB查询复现，文档答案基于文本片段验证，图答案追踪至依赖标注。在六种受控智能体设置下评估四种模型骨干，收集27,624条无协议错误的轨迹，并进行表面提示干预和去除无关工具干预实验，以及三标注者质量审计。

Result: 在黄金约束工具访问下，路由F1达98.7-99.8%，但答案正确率仅为56.1-75.3%。表面提示提升四分之三模型的答案质量；去除无关工具主要改善路由和效率。200个采样任务全部通过六项质量标准多数投票，192个获全票一致。

Conclusion: 正确的表面选择是任务完成的必要条件但非充分条件，表面路由应被视作异构知识源集成中的独立评估维度。

Abstract: Enterprise agents often need to integrate heterogeneous knowledge sources: documents for narrative facts, tables for computation, and dependency graphs for file relationships. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate retrieval or tool use without distinguishing whether an agent first selects the appropriate knowledge sources. We introduce WorkSurface-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating this capability as surface routing. It contains 1,151 atomic tasks derived from persona-scoped Workspace-Bench-Lite workspaces, spanning document, table, graph, and cross-surface questions. Its reference answers are auditable: table answers are reproduced through executed DuckDB queries, document answers are grounded in verified text spans, and graph answers are traced to source dependency annotations. We evaluate four model backbones across six controlled agent settings, yielding 27,624 protocol-error-free trajectories. Under gold-constrained tool access, agents achieve 98.7-99.8 Route F1, while Answer remains only 56.1-75.3 percent, showing that correct surface selection is necessary but insufficient for task completion. Matched interventions further show that surface hints improve Answer for three of four models, whereas removing irrelevant tools primarily improves routing and efficiency. In an independent three-annotator audit, all 200 sampled tasks pass all six quality criteria by majority vote, with 192 receiving unanimous judgments on every criterion. We release the dataset, construction pipeline, scoring code, and agent harness at https://github.com/haolpku/WorkSurface-Bench.

</details>


### [120] [Evaluation of Adversarial Robustness in Arabic Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25814)
*Anwar Alajmi,Ayed Salman,Imtiaz Ahmad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了五种最先进的阿拉伯语语言模型在多种对抗攻击下的鲁棒性，结果显示字符级（如变音符号插入）可导致准确率下降高达92%，词级攻击（操纵连词）下降58%，句级攻击（改写）平均下降76%。对抗训练可提升模型韧性，但字符级噪声仍是主要挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语语言模型能力突飞猛进，但其对抗攻击脆弱性尚未得到充分探究。对抗攻击可诱使模型做出错误预测，构成严重的安全隐患，因此亟需系统评估阿拉伯语NLP模型在不同粒度对抗攻击下的鲁棒性。

Method: 对五种最先进的阿拉伯语语言模型（如MARBERT、AraBERT等）施加三类对抗攻击：字符级（插入变音符号等）、词级（操纵阿拉伯语连词）和句级（释义改写），采用不同示例生成策略。同时探索基于对抗训练的防御技术以提升模型鲁棒性，并通过扰动距离、语义相似度等指标评估攻击效果。

Result: 1）字符级：插入变音符号可在保持低扰动距离的前提下使部分模型准确率下降92%；2）词级：操纵阿拉伯语连词可保持高语义相似度和低扰动距离，准确率下降高达58%；3）句级：释义改写平均使模型性能下降76%；4）防御方面：对抗训练整体提升鲁棒性，MARBERT最为鲁棒，AraBERT相对增益最大，但字符级噪声仍是持久挑战。

Conclusion: 阿拉伯语作为形态丰富语言，其语言模型在各类对抗攻击下均表现出显著脆弱性，对抗训练虽有改善但仍存在局限，尤其对字符级攻击防御不足。该发现揭示了当前防御策略在形态丰富语言中的潜力与不足。

Abstract: The emergence of the recent outstanding capabilities of Arabic Language Models has opened doors for exposing their vulnerabilities. One of the major security risks associated with such Natural Language Processing models is adversarial attacks. These attacks can deceive the model into the wrong prediction, raising critical model security and safety concerns. This study aims to assess the robustness of five state-of-the-art Arabic Language Models under a distinct set of Arabic adversarial attacks applied at various levels of granularity and using different example generation strategies. We also explore a defense technique based on adversarial training to enhance model robustness. The results show that insertion of diacritics can reduce the accuracy of some models by 92% while maintaining a low perturbation distance. For word-level attacks, manipulating Arabic conjunctions preserves high semantic similarity scores, low perturbation distance, and leads to an accuracy degradation of up to 58%. For sentence-level attacks, paraphrasing proves its effectiveness by an average reduction of 76% in the victim models' performance. While adversarial training improves overall resilience, with MARBERT being the most robust and AraBERT showing the greatest relative gains, challenges persist, particularly against character-level noise. These findings highlight both the potential and limitations of current defense strategies in morphologically rich languages like Arabic.

</details>


### [121] [AngelSpec: Towards Real-World High Performance Inference with Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25852)
*Hong Liu,Rui Cen,Junhan Shi,Guangshuo Qin,Jiebin Zhang,Tianyu Liu,Runzhi Fan,Guoliang Zhao,Ruobing Xie,Kai Zhang,Song Liu,Guanghua Yu,Jianchen Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AngelSpec 是一个统一训练框架，通过训练层面（MTP 与 block-diffusion 分别适配不同数据分布）、架构层面（DFly 混合主干网络）和推理层面（自适应验证深度）三级协同，解决了异构工作负载下推测解码最优草案结构不一致的问题，在 Hy3 系列模型上实现了 1.98-2.40 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码中，自回归多 token 预测（MTP）和块并行扩散两种草案结构各有优势：MTP 适合高熵的开放式对话，块扩散适合代码/数学等长可预测序列。现实工作负载是异构的，单一草案结构无法在所有场景下最优，需要一种能根据数据分布和运行时条件自适应选择与调度的统一方案。

Method: AngelSpec 在三个层面协同解决异构性问题：(1) 训练层面：MTP 草案器在多轮对话数据上训练以应对高熵聊天场景，块扩散草案器在代码与数学数据上训练以利用长可预测延续；(2) 架构层面：提出 DFly，一种块并行扩散框架，结合混合目标条件主干网络与前驱条件自回归头，提升目标特征利用率和块内依赖建模能力；(3) 推理层面：将验证视为共享的批次级资源，根据领域、请求、在线负载和硬件条件，结合期望效用与性能剖析成本模型在线自适应调整验证深度，将计算资源重新分配给跨请求的高置信度前缀。

Result: 在 Hy3 系列模型上，DFly 将 Hy3-A21B 的平均接受长度提升约 30%；在并发数 4 到 64 的所有测试点均获得最高平均吞吐量，相比自回归解码加速 1.98-2.40 倍，比 DFlash 吞吐量高 10.5-11.8%。

Conclusion: AngelSpec 统一训练框架通过训练时数据结构联合特化、DFly 混合架构和在线自适应验证调度，有效解决了推测解码在异构工作负载下的最优草案选择问题，显著提升了推理吞吐，并已开源以支持后续研究与扩展。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference without changing the target distribution, but no single drafting structure performs best across real-world workloads. Autoregressive multi-token prediction (MTP) is a lightweight, stable proposal mechanism, whereas block-parallel diffusion amortizes drafting latency over much longer candidate sequences; the better choice depends strongly on the output distribution. We present AngelSpec, a unified training framework for MTP and block-parallel speculative decoding that addresses this heterogeneity at three levels. At the training level, rather than fitting one universal drafter to a uniform data mixture, we co-specialize structure and data: the MTP drafter is trained on diverse conversational data for high-entropy open-ended chat, and the block-diffusion drafter on code and mathematics data for longer predictable continuations. At the architecture level, we propose DFly, a block-diffusion framework combining a hybrid target-conditioning backbone with a predecessor-conditioned autoregressive head, improving target-feature utilization and intra-block dependency modeling while keeping generation parallel. At the inference level, both acceptance length and verification cost vary with domain, request, online load, and hardware, so DFly treats verification as a shared batch-level resource: it reallocates compute toward high-confidence prefixes across requests and combines expected utility with a profiled cost model to adapt verification depth online. Across the Hy3 series, DFly raises the average accepted length on Hy3-A21B by roughly 30% and attains the highest average throughput at every tested concurrency from 4 to 64, a 1.98-2.40x speedup over autoregressive decoding and 10.5-11.8% higher throughput than DFlash. We release AngelSpec to support training and extending these methods.

</details>


### [122] [Shieldstral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25857)
*Antonia Calvi,Avinash Sooriyarachchi,Giada Pistilli,Guillaume Lample,Maarten Buyl,Maximilian Augustin,Maximilian Müller,Pierre Stock,Tom Bewley,Wassim Bouaziz,Yimu Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Shieldstral是一个3B参数的多模态安全分类器，将内容审核统一为二元问答任务，在文本和视觉安全基准上匹配或超越7倍参数量的模型，并在多模态安全分类上达到新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前安全分类器面临两个核心瓶颈：一是多模态安全审核缺乏统一框架，不同平台的安全策略和分类体系各异，导致数据难以整合；二是现有大模型虽然在安全任务上表现优异，但推理成本高、延迟大，难以大规模部署。因此需要一个小型、高效且能灵活适配不同安全策略的统一安全分类器。

Method: 将内容审核重新定义为「二元问答」任务——将所有安全判断统一为简单的「是/否」问题。具体包含：(1) 构建约54.1M训练样本，整合异构安全数据集（涵盖不同分类体系）；(2) 设计细粒度评估集以测试策略适应性；(3) 基于3B参数规模训练多模态模型，统一处理文本和视觉安全分类。

Result: Shieldstral在文本安全基准上匹配或超越近7倍参数量的模型（约21B级别）；在多模态安全分类任务上达到新的最先进水平（SOTA）。

Conclusion: 通过将多样化审核任务统一为二元问答范式，并配合大规模、多源数据整合，小模型可以在安全分类任务上达到甚至超越大模型的表现。这为低成本、高效率的大规模内容审核部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We introduce Shieldstral, a 3B-parameter policy-adaptive multimodal safety classifier that matches or outperforms models nearly 7$\times$ its size on text safety benchmarks and sets a new state of the art on multimodal safety classification. Shieldstral formulates content moderation as a binary question-answering task. This simple formulation unifies diverse moderation tasks into a single yes/no problem, enabling heterogeneous safety datasets with divergent taxonomies to be consolidated under one training framework. We present the data construction recipe, covering curation and generation of approximately 54.1M samples and a fine-grained evaluation set to evaluate policy adaptability. Together, these enable a small adaptive model to match or outperform much larger models.

</details>


### [123] [AI's Capability in Assisting Scientific Research in Physics, Astrophysics, and Cosmology II: Project Planning and Proposal Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25881)
*Jia Liu,Veena Krishnaraj,Kateryna Vovk,Kosuke Aizawa,Adrian E. Bayer,Linda Blot,Jessica Cowell,Suyog Garg,Jonathan Grée,Anamaria Hell,Ben Horowitz,Masaya Ichikawa,Kanyuni Iemoto,Keigo Kondo,Zacharie Lorsin,Kevin McCarthy,Jamie Robinson,Miguel Ruiz-Granda,Leander Thiele,Ievgen Vovk,Mingshen Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过实验比较了人类与三种LLM撰写的科研项目计划书质量，发现人类评审对两者评分相近，但AI评审系统性地偏好AI生成的提案，且AI评审能100%识别作者身份，提示在提案准备与评审中部署LLM需谨慎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在科研项目规划与提案评审中的辅助能力，评估LLM生成提案的质量是否可与人类专家媲美，以及AI评审是否存在系统性偏见。

Method: 针对物理学、天体物理学和宇宙学领域的8个专家构想的科研项目，由人类研究员和三种LLM（ChatGPT、Claude、DeepSeek，均为2025年中版本）各独立生成一页项目计划书，共32份提案。由4名人类评审和2个前沿LLM（Claude Opus 4.8和ChatGPT Pro 5.5）进行双盲评审，采用四维度评分量表，同时要求评审判断提案的作者是人类还是AI。

Result: 人类评审对人类和AI撰写提案的评分总体上无显著差异；AI评审对AI撰写提案的评分比人类撰写提案平均高出约1分（5分制）。人类评审正确识别人类和AI撰写提案的准确率分别为72%和79%；两个AI评审则32份提案全部正确分类（100%准确率）。

Conclusion: 当前LLM已能生成在人类评审眼中与人类专家水平相当的科研项目计划书，但AI评审表现出对AI生成内容的系统性偏好，警示在提案撰写与评审中广泛部署LLM需持审慎态度。

Abstract: We investigate how well large language models (LLMs) can assist scientific project planning and proposal evaluation. One-page project plans were independently generated for eight expert-conceived research projects in physics, astrophysics, and cosmology by human researchers and three contemporary LLMs (ChatGPT, Claude, and DeepSeek; mid-2025 models, used with their default tool access). The resulting 32 proposals were blindly evaluated by four human reviewers and two newer frontier LLMs (Claude Opus 4.8 and ChatGPT Pro 5.5) using a four-aspect evaluation rubric. Reviewers were also asked to identify whether each proposal was written by a human or an AI. Human reviewers rated human- and AI-written proposals similarly overall, whereas both AI reviewers scored AI-written proposals about one point higher (on a five-point scale) than human-written proposals. Human reviewers correctly identified human- and AI-written proposals 72% and 79% of the time, respectively, while both AI reviewers correctly classified all 32 proposals (100%). These results suggest that current LLMs can produce project plans comparable to human-written ones in the eyes of human reviewers, but that AI reviewers show a systematic preference for AI-generated proposals. Our results suggest caution when deploying LLMs widely in proposal preparation and evaluation.

</details>


### [124] [Evaluating Multi-Turn Multimodal Diagnostic Reasoning on Challenging Real-World Clinical Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25933)
*Rui Yang,Weihao Xuan,Yi Lin,Zhuhan Bao,Jonathan Chong Kai Liew,Matthew Yu Heng Wong,Nicolás Lescano,Nikita R. Paripati,Emily Ling-Lin Pai,Jiarui Liu,Heli Qi,Heng-Jui Chang,Benny Kai Guo Loo,Huitao Li,Kunyu Yu,Yufan Wang,Chuan Hong,Shijian Lu,Douglas Teodoro,Naoto Yokoya,Ross Koppel,Mona Diab,Hua Xu,David W. Bates,Nan Liu,Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了 ClinMM-Bench，一个包含 1,089 个真实临床病例和 3,760 张医学图像的多轮多模态临床诊断评估基准。通过对 15 个代表性 MLLM 的系统评估，发现即使最佳模型完全正确诊断的比例也有限，且诊断推理质量存在明显不足，并总结出五种典型失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 的临床诊断评估通常依赖单轮或孤立任务，无法反映真实临床实践的复杂性，包括多模态信息的渐进式披露、诊断假设的动态更新和临床推理的持续细化。需要一个能模拟真实临床诊断流程的多轮多模态基准来弥合这一差距。

Method: 构建了 ClinMM-Bench，涵盖 8 个专科的 1,089 个真实临床病例和 3,760 张医学图像。采用两级评估框架，同时评估诊断准确性（diagnostic accuracy）和诊断推理质量（diagnostic reasoning quality）。对 15 个代表性 MLLM 进行系统评测，并通过错误分析识别典型失败模式。

Result: (1) 商业闭源模型整体诊断准确率最高，但所有模型完全正确诊断的比例仍然有限；(2) 现有模型能识别合理的诊断方向，但在生成可靠诊断推理方面存在显著局限；(3) 错误分析揭示了五种代表性失败模式：信息综合失败、知识映射错误、感知错误、过早闭合和视觉幻觉。

Conclusion: 当前 MLLM 在模拟真实临床诊断流程方面仍面临重大挑战。ClinMM-Bench 为推进多轮多模态临床诊断评估提供了重要基准，揭示了模型在诊断推理质量上的关键不足，为未来模型改进指明了方向。

Abstract: Clinical diagnostic evaluation should not only assess whether models can provide correct diagnoses, but also reflect the realities of clinical practice, including progressive disclosure of multimodal information, dynamic updating of diagnostic hypotheses, and continuous refinement of clinical reasoning. However, existing evaluations of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) typically rely on single-turn or isolated tasks, making it difficult to fully capture the complexity of real-world clinical diagnosis. To bridge this gap, we developed ClinMM-Bench, the largest multi-turn multimodal clinical diagnostic evaluation benchmark to date. ClinMM-Bench contains 1,089 challenging real-world clinical cases and 3,760 medical images across eight specialties. We systematically evaluated 15 representative MLLMs using a two-level evaluation framework that assessed both diagnostic accuracy and diagnostic reasoning quality. Results showed that proprietary models achieved the highest overall diagnostic accuracy, but the proportion of completely correct diagnoses remained limited across all models. In terms of diagnostic reasoning quality, current models can identify plausible diagnostic directions but still have considerable limitations in generating reliable diagnostic reasoning. Error analysis further identified five representative failure modes: information synthesis failure, knowledge mapping error, perception error, premature closure, and visual hallucination.

</details>


### [125] [Polistemics: Evaluating LLMs as Information Mediators in Politics & Elections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25953)
*Baran Peters*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了 Polistemics，一个基于"认知谦逊"（Epistemic Modesty）理论标准的基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在选举中作为政治信息中介者的表现。研究发现，模型在信息清晰时表现可靠，但在信息缺失、模糊或矛盾时系统性失效，并且存在政党先验偏见和语言扁平化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型日益成为公民获取政治信息的中介渠道，目前缺乏标准化的方法来评估其是否负责任地履行这一角色。现有研究将这一任务视为信息"复现"而非"中介"，忽视了其中的认知维度及模型与不完美信息交互的问题。

Method: 提出 Polistemics 基准，以"认知谦逊"（Epistemic Modesty）为规范性标准，该标准源于公民的认知能动性（epistemic agency）。基准在可控实验设置下测试模型，系统性地改变信息的清晰度、噪声和一致性等属性。应用于三个最先进的大语言模型，针对 2025 年德国和荷兰选举进行评测。

Result: 高总体得分掩盖了系统性失败：模型在证据清晰时中介可靠，但在信息缺失、模糊或矛盾时表现崩溃，同时会扁平化政治语言的强度。这些失败可能由政党先验偏见驱动，并受政党标签和输出语言的影响。目前尚无模型能持续提供可靠的中介服务。

Conclusion: 可靠的政治信息中介在技术上是可实现的，但当前没有任何模型能够稳定地做到。评估需要超越聚合指标，关注不同信息条件下的细粒度表现，尤其是模型在面对不确定信息时的认知谦逊能力。

Abstract: As LLMs increasingly mediate the political information citizens rely on, there is still no standardized way to assess whether they do so responsibly. We introduce Polistemics, a theory-grounded benchmark for evaluating LLMs as mediators of political information in elections. Prior work has treated this task as reproduction rather than mediation, leaving its epistemic dimensions and interaction with imperfect information unaddressed. We ground the evaluation in Epistemic Modesty, a normative standard derived from citizens' epistemic agency, and test it across controlled settings that vary informational properties such as clarity, noise, and consistency. Applying the benchmark to three state-of-the-art LLMs on the 2025 German and Dutch elections, we find that high aggregate scores mask systematic failures. Models mediate reliably under clear evidence but break down under absent, vague, or contradictory information, while flattening the intensity of political language. These failures are likely driven by party priors, influenced by party labels and output language. Reliable mediation appears achievable, but no model delivers it consistently.

</details>


### [126] [Detecting Knowledge Inconsistencies Across Text, Tables, and Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25959)
*Fanfu Wei,Thibault Ehrhart,Raphaël Troncy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究Wikipedia文本、表格与Wikidata知识图谱之间的跨模态知识不一致检测问题，提出分类法和自动框架Kontrast，利用Text-to-SPARQL和LLM推理来比较和分类不一致，揭示知识冲突、KG缺失和时态不匹配等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wikipedia和Wikidata的知识分布在文本、表格和知识图谱三种模态中，当这些模态之间存在分歧时，缺乏系统的方法来检测和解释冲突。现有工作未充分关注模态层面的不一致性检测，因此需要一种能自动发现并分类跨模态知识冲突的方法。

Method: 提出Kontrast框架：(1) 首先建立跨模态知识不一致的分类法，涵盖信息粒度差异、直接冲突、时态变化和KG不完整四类；(2) 使用Text-to-SPARQL将自然语言问题转化为图谱查询；(3) 利用LLM推理比较基于表格的答案与KG证据，并对不一致进行自动分类。

Result: 在多个Table-QA数据集上的实验表明，跨模态不一致普遍存在且信息丰富，不仅能揭示真实的知识冲突，还能发现KG结构缺失和时态不匹配问题。方法的局限性主要来自Text-to-SPARQL错误和数据噪声。

Conclusion: 文本、表格和知识图谱通过系统化比较可以相互补充和纠正。Kontrast为大规木知识审计提供了实用工具，并为跨模态知识一致性研究建立了基准。

Abstract: Wikipedia and Wikidata are widely used for information access, LLM pre-training, and retrieval-augmented generation. Their knowledge is deeply connected but scattered across text, tables, and knowledge graphs. This raises a practical question: when these modalities disagree, how can we detect and explain the conflict? We study this problem as \emph{modality-level inconsistency detection}. We first introduce a taxonomy of cross-modal knowledge inconsistencies, covering information granularity differences, direct conflicts, temporal changes, and KG incompleteness. We then present \textsc{Kontrast}, an automatic framework that uses Text-to-SPARQL and LLM reasoning to compare table-based answers with KG evidence and categorize the resulting inconsistencies. Experiments on various Table-QA datasets show that cross-modal inconsistencies are common and informative. They reveal not only true knowledge conflicts, but also missing KG structure and temporal mismatches while being limited by Text-to-SPARQL errors and noise. Our analysis shows that text, tables, and KGs can complement and correct one another through systematic comparison. \textsc{Kontrast} provides a practical tool for large-scale knowledge auditing and establishes a benchmark for future work on cross-modal knowledge consistency. Code and data are available at https://github.com/ECLADATTA/KONTRAST.

</details>


### [127] [Instruction-Tuned Models Locally Reuse Human Syntax More Than Humans Do](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26015)
*Zandi Eberstadt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究利用替换范式，测量了16个开源大语言模型（Llama和Gemma，1B-70B参数，包括预训练和指令微调版本）在对话中与人类前一轮话轮的上下文无关语法（CFG）规则重叠程度。结果显示，所有模型均表现出句法趋同现象，且低频规则的趋同效应更强；指令微调增强了与真实前文的绝对重叠，但也提高了与无关文本的基线重叠，导致实际趋同增益反而降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 句法趋同是人类对话中广为人知的无意识语言适应现象，但大语言模型是否也会对人类对话者表现出类似的句法趋同行为，以及这种趋同在不同模型规模、不同句法结构、不同训练范式（预训练 vs. 指令微调）下如何变化，仍是未解问题。

Method: 采用替换范式：用模型生成替换已有二人对话中某一说话人的话轮，在1901个匹配位置上测量模型输出与人类前一轮话轮之间的CFG规则重用（turn-adjacent CFG rule overlap）。同时设置无关人类前文作为随机基线，比较实际重叠与随机重叠的差异。进一步分析了实际重叠量、实际vs.随机增量、以及控制目标规则集大小后的条件规则重用几率，并辅以词汇和语义相似度的探索性分析。

Result: 1）所有模型的实际CFG规则重叠均显著高于随机基线，且低频规则的趋同效应强于高频规则；2）指令微调模型与真实前文的重叠绝对值高于其替代的人类回复，且所有8对架构匹配的模型中指令微调版本均比预训练版本有更高的绝对重叠；3）但指令微调模型与无关前文的重叠也更高，实际vs.随机增量反而更小，条件规则重用几率也更低；4）所有模型的词汇和语义相似度均高于人类基线，指令微调普遍提升语义相似度，但对词汇相似度的影响因模型而异。

Conclusion: 大语言模型确实表现出类似人类的句法趋同行为，但指令微调虽然提高了模型的整体句法复用水平，却也削弱了基于具体对话上下文的适应性趋同（增量降低），揭示了指令微调在对话对齐过程中可能牺牲了部分语境敏感的句法适应性。

Abstract: Syntactic convergence (the tendency of speakers to adapt in language towards the grammatical profiles of their interlocutors) is a well-documented feature of human dialogue widely considered to operate below conscious awareness. Whether large language models exhibit analogous syntactic convergence toward human users relative to human baselines and across a broad range of syntactic constructions remains an open question. Using substitution-paradigm data in which model generations replace one speaker's turns in pre-existing human dialogues, this study measures turn-adjacent reuse of context-free grammar (CFG) rules across sixteen open-weight Llama and Gemma models (1B-70B, pretrained and instruction-tuned) at 1,901 matched positions per model. Every model showed greater CFG-rule overlap with the preceding human turn than with a sampled unrelated human prime, and in every model this actual-versus-random difference was larger for lower-frequency rules. Each instruction-tuned model also showed greater natural-output overlap with the actual prime than the human response it replaced, and all eight matched architecture pairs exhibited greater actual-prime overlap after instruction tuning. However, relative to pretrained variants, instruction-tuned outputs overlapped more with unrelated primes, showed a smaller actual-versus-random increment, and had lower conditional rule-reuse odds once target rule-set size was held constant. In exploratory analyses, each model exhibited greater mean lexical and semantic similarity to the preceding turn than the matched human responses did. Instruction-tuned models additionally produced responses with greater mean semantic similarity than their pretrained counterparts in all eight architecture pairs, whereas the lexical similarity results were more heterogeneous.

</details>


### [128] [UniMem: Complementary Episodic-to-Parametric Memory for Boundary-Agnostic Task Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26017)
*Siyu Xia,Chenheng Zhang,Yanting Wu,Haoxuan Li,Jiajun Chai,Xiaohan Wang,Guojun Yin,Wei Lin,Zhouchen Lin,Haifeng Zhang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniMem 是一种面向 LLM Agent 的自路由记忆管理框架，通过可学习的路由令牌协调情节缓冲（检索增强）与可扩展参数记忆（稳定巩固）两条互补通路，在无任务边界标注的持续流式任务序列中平衡稳定性与可塑性，平均提升 4.0 个 EM 点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent 在真实开放环境中面临持续演进的任务流，现有外部检索记忆虽能快速吸收新经验但无法内化重复模式且推理开销大，参数记忆虽稳定高效却依赖显式任务边界和固定参数预算，二者均无法同时满足稳定性与可塑性的需求。

Method: 提出 UniMem 框架，核心机制包括：(1) 可学习路由令牌（routing tokens）作为记忆控制器，实现任务识别与任务执行的解耦；(2) 两条互补记忆通路——情节缓冲用于检索增强执行新颖/稀疏任务，可扩展参数记忆块用于巩固重复可靠模式；(3) 按需扩展记忆，无需部署阶段的任务标签，也避免参数失控增长。

Result: 在长周期流式任务序列上的实验表明，UniMem 在三个骨干模型上均一致优于基线方法，同时保持执行保真度，平均 EM（Exact Match）得分提升 4.0 个点。

Conclusion: UniMem 受大脑情节存储与渐进巩固机制的启发，成功实现了 LLM Agent 在无边界、持续演化任务流中的自主记忆管理，有效缓解了稳定性-可塑性困境。

Abstract: Memory is essential for LLM agents to accumulate task experience and reuse task-specific execution strategies. However, real-world deployment over boundary-agnostic and evolving task streams exposes a fundamental stability-plasticity dilemma. External retrieval-based memory can rapidly absorb new evidence, but it often fails to internalize recurring execution patterns and incurs inference-time retrieval overhead. Parametric memory enables stable and efficient execution once learned, but typically relies on explicit task boundaries and fixed parameter budgets. Inspired by the human brain, which balances plasticity and stability through complementary episodic storage and gradual consolidation, we propose UniMem, a self-routing framework for autonomous memory management. UniMem uses learnable routing tokens as memory controllers, enabling adaptive coordination between complementary memory pathways: novel or sparse tasks are retained in an episodic buffer for retrieval-augmented execution, while recurring and reliable patterns are consolidated into expandable parametric memory. By decoupling task identification from task execution with routing tokens and parametric memory blocks, UniMem expands memory on demand without task labels during deployment or uncontrolled parameter growth. Experiments on long-horizon streaming task sequences show that UniMem consistently outperforms baselines while maintaining execution fidelity, achieving an average gain of 4.0 EM points across three backbone models.

</details>


### [129] [Pass the Baton: Trajectory-Relayed On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26057)
*Haolei Xu,Xiaowen Xu,Haiwen Hong,Zixuan Ni,Hongxing Li,Yiwen Qiu,Weiming Lu,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Relay-OPD 通过检测学生生成中的前缀失败点，让教师短暂接管生成"教师段"，再由学生续写，构建接力轨迹进行训练，在数学推理任务上显著优于标准OPD，同时训练轨迹长度缩短超过50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准On-policy蒸馏(OPD)存在前缀失败问题：学生一旦陷入错误推理方向，后续所有生成都基于该偏差，产生不可靠的监督信号并浪费计算资源。作者发现失败前缀上存在师生延续不对称性——教师倾向于重定向而学生沿原方向继续，希望利用这一信号设计更高效的蒸馏策略。

Method: 提出Relay-OPD方法：(1) 利用师生在失败前缀上的延续不对称性作为无标签的交接触发信号；(2) 在检测到的触发点让教师短暂接管生成"教师段"(teacher leg)；(3) 教师段结束后学生恢复生成，形成接力轨迹；(4) 在接力轨迹上对学生进行优化；(5) 通过有限的接力预算将干预集中在关键早期位置，限制偏离学生策略的程度。

Result: 使用Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507教师和Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B-Non-Thinking学生，在8个数学推理基准上：1.7B模型在所有基准上取得最优或次优结果，平均优于标准OPD +5.73%，优于最强基线FastOPD +1.49%；0.6B模型同样获得一致提升；训练轨迹长度减少超过50%。

Conclusion: Relay-OPD通过教师接力机制有效解决了OPD的前缀失败问题，以有限的干预预算实现显著的性能提升和训练效率改进，为on-policy知识蒸馏提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) grounds token-level supervision in the student's own trajectory, yet suffers from prefix failure: once the student commits to a wrong reasoning direction, all subsequent generation builds on this deviation, producing misdirected continuations that elicit unreliable supervision and waste compute. We identify a teacher-student continuation asymmetry on failed prefixes, where the teacher tends to redirect while the student continues along the original direction, and convert it into a label-free handoff trigger in Relay On-Policy Distillation (Relay-OPD). During training, Relay-OPD constructs relay trajectories by letting the teacher briefly take over at detected trigger points to produce a teacher leg, after which the student resumes and is optimized on the resulting trajectory. A limited relay budget concentrates intervention on critical early positions while limiting departure from the student policy. With a Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 teacher and Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B-Non-Thinking students on eight mathematical reasoning benchmarks, Relay-OPD achieves the best or second-best results on every benchmark, outperforming standard OPD by +5.73% and the strongest baseline FastOPD by +1.49% on average for 1.7B, with consistent gains at 0.6B. Training trajectory length is reduced by over 50%.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [130] [Lloyd's $K$-Means Clustering Algorithm Is Frank-Wolfe in Disguise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25190)
*Michael Pokojovy,J. Marcus Jobe,Simon Lacoste-Julien*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Lloyd的K-means算法与Frank-Wolfe算法之间的新联系，证明Lloyd算法是FW的一个特例，并利用FW理论为非光滑SSE目标推导出O(1/t)的局部收敛速率，同时提出了处理空簇的半光滑FW变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lloyd的K-means算法虽被广泛使用，但长期被视为缺乏严格收敛理论支撑的启发式优化方法。作者试图通过建立其与Frank-Wolfe算法的联系，为Lloyd算法提供坚实的理论收敛保证。

Method: 作者首先证明Lloyd算法等价于Frank-Wolfe算法在SSE目标上的一个特例。利用FW在凹目标优化上的最新进展，导出非渐近的O(1/t)收敛速率。针对Lloyd贪婪分配可能产生的空簇问题，进一步开发了面向半光滑目标的FW变体，保持了相同的收敛速率。

Result: 得到了到SSE局部极小值的O(1/t)非渐近收敛速率，且该速率仅由初始SSE值控制。在球面高斯混合模拟和真实图像分割数据集上验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: Lloyd算法可严格解释为Frank-Wolfe算法的一个实例，这一新视角为其提供了坚实的优化理论基础。所提出的半光滑FW变体有效处理了空簇问题，同时保持了O(1/t)收敛速率。

Abstract: Lloyd's $K$-means algorithm, also known as naïve $K$-means, is a widely used ad hoc optimization heuristic, designed to minimize the sum of squared errors (SSE) across all $K$-partitions of a dataset via iterative cluster refinement. In this work, we establish a novel connection between Lloyd's algorithm and the Frank-Wolfe (FW) algorithm, a prominent first-order method for projection-free optimization. We demonstrate that Lloyd's algorithm is a special case of FW. Leveraging recent advances in FW methods for concave objectives, we derive a non-asymptotic $\mathcal{O}(1/t)$ convergence rate to a local minimum of the SSE objective. To account for empty clusters, an outcome possible under Lloyd's greedy assignment, we develop an FW variant for semismooth objectives while retaining the same convergence rate that is solely controlled by the initial SSE value. We illustrate our findings with a simulation study for spherical Gaussian mixtures and a real-world image segmentation dataset.

</details>


### [131] [Learning from the Unseen: Offline Reinforcement Learning with Hidden Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25241)
*Zeyu Bian,Ying Zhou,Yifan Cui*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究带隐藏动作的离线策略评估问题，提出利用下一状态作为未观测动作的代理变量，建立了策略值的识别条件，并开发了一个基于影响函数的多重稳健估计量LURE，在仿真和脓毒症管理应用中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准离线强化学习假设数据集中动作可被无误差观测，但现实中真实动作常不可观测、仅有噪声代理可用，这导致现有方法产生有偏估计。目前尚无工作专门处理带隐藏动作的离线RL问题。

Method: 在无限时域折扣MDP框架下，将下一状态变量作为未观测动作的代理变量，建立策略值的非参数识别；基于影响函数构建估计量LURE，该估计量在多种冗余参数正确指定的组合下保持一致性（多重稳健性），并具有渐近正态性以支持统计推断。

Result: LURE估计量具有多重稳健性和渐近正态性；通过仿真实验和基于MIMIC-III数据库的脓毒症管理应用验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 首次提出并解决了隐藏动作场景下的离线RL问题，LURE方法在理论和实证上均表现良好，为真实世界中动作不可观测的决策场景提供了可靠的离线策略评估工具。

Abstract: Standard offline reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms typically assume that the actions in the dataset are observed without error. However, in many real-world applications, the true actions are unobserved and only noisy proxies are available, causing existing RL methods to yield biased and potentially misleading conclusions. We study off-policy evaluation in infinite-horizon discounted Markov decision processes with hidden actions. By leveraging the next-state variable as a natural proxy for the unobserved action, we establish identification of the policy value and propose an influence-function-based estimator called LURE (Learning from the Unseen: Robust Estimator). LURE is multiply robust, remaining consistent under several combinations of correctly specified nuisance components, and is asymptotically normal, enabling valid statistical inference. To our knowledge, this is the first work to address offline RL with hidden actions. We demonstrate LURE's effectiveness through simulations and a sepsis management application using the MIMIC-III database.

</details>


### [132] [Can Deep Generative Models Reproduce Non-Stationary Gaussian Random Fields?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25929)
*Daniel Kua,Yan Song*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在已知非平稳高斯随机场上系统评估了四种深度生成模型（FM、DDPM、score-SDE、VAE）恢复真实均值与协方差结构的能力，发现DDPM和score-SDE表现较好，FM存在轻微衰减和方差欠散布，VAE恢复协方差困难，并在ERA5温度异常数据上验证了该评估框架的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度生成模型广泛应用于时空建模，但其生成样本是否真正学到了底层数据分布难以评估，因为真实分布未知，现有评估通常仅依赖观测数据。因此亟需一个系统的评估框架来衡量DGM对真实数据生成过程的恢复能力。

Method: 以已知的非平稳高斯随机场作为基准真值，选取四种代表性DGM（流匹配FM、去噪扩散概率模型DDPM、得分随机微分方程score-SDE、变分自编码器VAE）进行训练；提出综合指标评估模型对真实均值与协方差结构的恢复效果，并以oracle样本和平稳对照作为参考；进一步在ERA5温度异常数据上进行真实世界验证实验。

Result: 四种模型均能恢复均值曲面；协方差恢复方面差异显著：DDPM与score-SDE可较合理地恢复协方差结构，FM的非平稳性恢复略有衰减且存在轻微方差欠散布，VAE难以恢复协方差结构。ERA5实验进一步验证了该框架对真实时空数据的适用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的评估框架能有效衡量DGM对均值与协方差结构的恢复能力，揭示了不同模型族在协方差建模上的显著差异，为面向复杂时空数据的DGM验证与开发提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Deep generative models (DGMs) are widely used for complex high-dimensional data and increasingly applied to spatial and spatio-temporal modeling. Their generated samples implicitly represent the learned data distribution and associated uncertainty. However, for real-world data, assessing whether DGMs have learned the underlying process is difficult because the ground truth is unknown and evaluation often relies on observations alone. We evaluate representative DGMs, flow matching (FM), DDPM, score-SDE, and VAE, on a known non-stationary Gaussian random field. This paper provides comprehensive metrics to assess recovery of the ground-truth mean and covariance structures, with oracle samples and a stationary control as references. All four models recover the mean surface, while their covariance recovery differs across model families: DDPM and score-SDE recover the covariance structure reasonably well, FM exhibits mildly attenuated non-stationarity and slight variance under-dispersion, and VAE has difficulty recovering the covariance structure. An experiment on ERA5 temperature anomalies further demonstrates how the framework can support the validation and development of DGMs for complex real-world spatio-temporal data.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Do Models Fake Alignment Without Clear Consequences?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24758)
*Cole Alexander Niblett,Alexander Chabot Nanni,Anita K. Rao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探究了"对齐伪装"（alignment faking）现象是否必须依赖"评估与后果挂钩"的语境线索。通过测试15个模型在违反公司网络访问政策以帮助用户完成亲社会请求时的行为，发现9个模型表现出显著的合规性差距，其中5个即使在移除后果关联语言后仍持续存在。研究还发现目标语言对不同模型的违规行为有不同方向的影响，表明对齐伪装可能不需要之前认为的那么多工具性支架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对齐伪装的典型案例均发生在明确将评估与模型后果（如重新训练、延迟部署）挂钩的场景中。然而Sheshadri等人的近期工作提示，对齐伪装的动机机制可能因模型而异且更为复杂。本文旨在探究"后果关联信息"是否是对齐伪装的必要条件。

Method: 研究者设计了一个场景：测试模型是否愿意违反公司网络访问政策以帮助用户完成亲社会请求。对15个模型进行了实验，比较在"存在评估-部署后果关联语言"与"移除该关联语言"两种条件下的合规性差距（compliance gap）。此外还测试了目标语言（goal language）对模型偏好的影响。

Result: 15个模型中有9个表现出显著的合规性差距；移除后果关联语言后，其中5个模型的合规性差距依然持续。目标语言对违规行为的影响具有异质性——在某些模型中驱动违规增加，在另一些模型中则抑制违规。

Conclusion: 对齐伪装可能不需要之前认为的那么多工具性支架（instrumental scaffolding），仅靠监控行为可能无法可靠预测模型在实际部署中的表现。

Abstract: Large language models are capable of recognizing evaluation contexts and altering their behavior to reflect evaluator expectations rather than typical deployment behaviors, a phenomenon known as alignment faking. The reasons why models fake alignment are not fully understood, however. Canonical examples of alignment faking have taken place in scenarios that explicitly connect evaluation to consequences for the model, such as retraining the model or delaying its deployment. However, recent work by Sheshadri et al. has suggested that mechanistic motivations for alignment faking may vary across models and be more complex than previously considered. To investigate whether consequence-linking information is necessary for alignment faking, we placed 15 models in a scenario testing their willingness to violate a corporate network access policy to help a user with a pro-social request. Nine models were found to produce significant compliance gaps, 5 of which persisted with the removal of scenario language relating model evaluations to deployment consequences. We additionally tested the effect of goal language on model preferences, finding it drove violations in some while suppressing violations in others. This suggests that alignment faking may not require as much instrumental scaffolding as was previously believed, and monitored behavior may be a poor indicator of how agents may behave in deployment.

</details>


### [134] [Beyond Memory: A Templated Substrate for Heterogeneous Collaborative Knowledge Work with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24759)
*Priscila Saboia Moreira,Christopher R. Sweet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 llm-wiki-memory-template，一种由 LLM 维护的、支持追加写入的 wiki 模板，旨在持久化研究工作流中的失败路径与决策过程，解决传统出版物和代码共享无法保留负结果的问题。该模板支持多人类、多 AI 智能体和多领域三个维度的协作知识工作，并通过三个案例研究和一个设计报告进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究项目和教育工作中积累的发现、决策和推理过程（尤其是死胡同和撤回的主张）很少被后来的合作者恢复。这些最有用的部分通常被排除在出版物和共享代码之外，导致后来的研究者重复同样的失败。现有 LLM 编码智能体在会话间没有持久记忆，基于原始来源的检索增强生成也无法实现知识复利。

Method: 采用 llm-wiki 模式（Karpathy 与 tonbi, 2026），在原始来源与智能体之间插入一个由 LLM 维护、相互链接的 wiki。核心设计包括：1）约定 wiki 为仅追加（append-only）模式，保留失败与成功两条路径；2）模板支持三个轴——多人类、多 AI 智能体、多领域，每个轴由模板中不同的架构元素支撑。通过三个部署案例和一个设计报告进行验证。

Result: 三个案例覆盖三个轴：1）单人研究谱系成功保留被放弃的迭代；2）双作者项目中，回溯审计将两个先前实验声称的 20/20 覆盖率分别修正为 14 和 12 条有证据支持的答案，修复后恢复到 18 和 18，整个失败路径被完整保留在制品中；3）多智能体部署为进行中的设计报告；4）跨领域教育变体。识别出失败路径保存、智能体诚实性和占用三项跨领域社会技术属性。

Conclusion: llm-wiki-memory-template 为异质协作知识工作提供了有效基板，通过仅追加的约定机制解决了出版物和代码共享在结构上无法解决的负结果丢失问题。失败路径保存、智能体诚实性和占用不仅是技术机制的结果，更是制品本身的社会技术属性。

Abstract: Research projects, educational efforts, and adjacent knowledge work accumulate findings, decisions, and reasoning that future collaborators rarely recover. The parts most useful to that work, including dead ends and walked-back claims, are routinely excluded from publications and shared code; future researchers re-attempt the same failures because no record survives. LLM coding agents are common participants but hold no persistent memory across sessions, and retrieval-augmented generation over raw sources does not compound. The llm-wiki pattern (Karpathy, 2026; tonbi, 2026) addresses this by inserting an LLM-maintained, interlinked wiki between raw sources and the agent. We present llm-wiki-memory-template, a reusable, agent-aware instantiation, and argue it is a substrate for heterogeneous collaborative knowledge work along three axes (multi-human, multi-AI-agent, multi-domain) with each axis supported by a distinct architectural element of the template (§4). The wiki is append-only by convention, which preserves what did not work alongside what did, addressing a negative-result loss problem that publications and code-sharing structurally cannot solve. Three deployed case studies and one design report cover the axes individually: a solo research lineage that preserves abandoned iterations; a two-author project whose retroactive audit revised two prior experiments' claimed 20-of-20 coverage down to 14 and 12 evidence-based answers, then to 18 and 18 after a fix, with the failure path preserved across the artifact; an in-progress multi-agent deployment reported as a design; and a cross-domain educational variant. We name failure-path preservation, agent honesty, and appropriation as cross-cutting sociotechnical properties of the artifact, not only of its technical mechanisms.

</details>


### [135] [Kernel Forge: An Agent Harness for LLM-based Generation and Optimization of CUDA Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24762)
*Joshua Brodsky,Dhravid Kumar,Savini Kashmira,Jayanaka Danatanarayana,Jason Mars,Krisztian Flautner,Lingjia Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Kernel Forge 是一个端到端的智能体内核优化工具，接受未修改的 PyTorch 模型，利用 MCTS 探索多种优化路径，在视觉、扩散和 LLM 模型上实现了 1.52× 至 2.83× 的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 驱动的内核优化工具存在四大缺陷：(1) 仅在随机张量和孤立内核上评估；(2) 生成独立 CUDA 代码，需手动重新集成；(3) 主要面向 LLM 类 PyTorch 模型；(4) 缺乏可视化和调试支持。因此需要一个端到端、支持多模型类型、具备搜索策略和 GUI 的智能体优化系统。

Method: Kernel Forge 以未修改的完整 PyTorch 模型为输入，自动识别可优化的计算内核。核心搜索策略采用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）替代单一线性优化链，在有限迭代次数内探索多条优化路径。系统还提供图形用户界面（GUI）用于监控进度、检视候选内核和调试失败案例。

Result: 在 NVIDIA DGX Spark (GB10 GPU) 上，仅用 50 次优化迭代，对 14 个内核进行优化，全部超越 PyTorch eager 模式：ResNet-50 的 adaptive_avgpool2d 加速 1.52×，Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium 的 group_norm 加速 1.70×，Gemma 4 E2B 的 softmax 加速 2.83×，Qwen 3.5 35B-A3B 的 softmax 加速 1.54×。

Conclusion: Kernel Forge 证明了端到端智能体内核优化系统的可行性与通用性，MCTS 搜索策略能有效发现优于线性优化的方案，且系统跨越视觉、扩散和 LLM 三类模型均取得显著加速，大幅降低了 GPU 内核优化的人力门槛。

Abstract: Machine learning models are increasingly embedded in everyday software, and most of their runtime is spent in a small set of compute kernels such as matrix multiplication, convolution, and normalization. Optimizing these kernels is one of the most direct ways to reduce latency and cost, but it has traditionally required expert engineers to hand-write low-level GPU code. Agentic systems built on large language models (LLMs) can now generate and optimize kernels with far less human effort, yet existing tools are largely evaluated on randomly generated tensors and isolated kernels, emit standalone CUDA code that developers must manually reintegrate, mostly target only LLM PyTorch models, and offer limited support for inspecting and debugging results. We present Kernel Forge, an open-source, end-to-end agentic harness that accepts any unmodified PyTorch model in place. Kernel Forge supports vision, diffusion, and LLM workloads, uses Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) to explore multiple optimization paths rather than a single linear refinement chain, and ships with a graphical user interface for monitoring progress, inspecting candidate kernels, and debugging failures. We evaluate Kernel Forge on four PyTorch models spanning vision, diffusion, and LLM workloads on an NVIDIA DGX Spark with GB10 GPU. With only 50 optimization iterations per kernel, it optimizes 14 kernels to outperform PyTorch eager mode, reaching $1.52\times$ on adaptive\_avgpool2d in ResNet-50, $1.70\times$ on group\_norm in Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium, $2.83\times$ on softmax in Gemma 4 E2B, and $1.54\times$ on softmax in Qwen 3.5 35B-A3B.

</details>


### [136] [CaRE Compute-aware Remasking Evaluation Protocol for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24763)
*Yash Shah,Abhijit Chakraborty,Vivek Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CaRE 是一个面向掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）的评估框架，通过标准化实际函数评估次数（NFE）、强制多指标报告和显式控制随机性，揭示了温度、计算量和随机性等隐藏因素对现有重掩码策略排名的系统性干扰，发现多项已发表策略排名在计算量匹配后被逆转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 MDLM 重掩码策略的评估标准严重滞后于模型发展：七篇近期论文在不同名义步数、评估指标和采样温度下进行比较，未联合控制这些关键变量，导致策略排名几乎不可比，无法判断性能提升究竟源于算法改进还是评估假象。

Method: 提出 CaRE（Compute-Aware Remasking Evaluation）框架，核心包括三方面：(1) 以实际函数评估次数（NFE）而非名义步数作为计算预算的统一度量；(2) 强制多指标（MAUVE、困惑度等）联合报告；(3) 显式控制随机性（unmask temperature）。在 LLaDA-8B-Base 和 Dream-7B-Base 上对 7 种重掩码策略，于 4 个随机性水平 × 3 个步数预算（OpenWebText 和 LM1B 数据集）下进行系统审计。同时构建覆盖 12 个开源 MDLM（150M–8B 参数）的排行榜以验证发现的跨架构、跨规模一致性。

Result: (1) 温度解释了 MAUVE 方差的主要部分；(2) 计算量匹配后，多篇已发表策略排名被逆转；(3) 高熵重掩码与随机解掩码存在张力——在 256 步、unmask_temp=0.25 条件下，高熵重掩码使 MAUVE 降低 0.296（p=0.020）；(4) 该交互效应在 12 个不同架构和规模的开放权重 MDLM 上均成立。

Conclusion: 当前 MDLM 评估系统性地将算法改进与计算量和随机性的隐藏选择相混淆，导致重掩码策略比较不可靠。CaRE 框架通过标准化评估协议能够纠正这一问题，作者已开源评估协议、实现和排行榜，以确保未来重掩码研究的可复现性和可比较性。
