We show that this follows from an invariance property of the objective: ejection fraction is a ratio and is unchanged when both volumes are rescaled, so the loss determines the concept layer only up to scale. Supervision in absolute units reduced volume error from 89.8 to 25.8 millilitres at a cost of 0.4 in ejection fraction error.
  Conclusion: Concept accuracy alone can conceal a concept layer that carries no physical scale.
  Significance: Interpretable intermediate variables in clinical models should be validated against the invariance structure of the training objective, not only against prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [233] [Speculate While You Reason: Teaching Agents to Predict Their Next Tool Call via Joint Agent-Speculator RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25816)
*Jiabao Ji,Yujian Liu,Li An,Rohit Jain,Gungor Polatkan,Siyu Zhu,Shiyu Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"自推测智能体"（self-speculating agent），将智能体与其工具调用推测器统一在同一模型中，通过联合强化学习训练，在不降低任务成功率的前提下显著提升下一步工具调用的预测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体在等待工具调用结果时存在大量延迟。现有工具调用推测方法（如独立的草稿模型或缓存轨迹）与部署智能体的行为对齐不佳，存在"推测器-智能体鸿沟"（speculator-agent gap）。作者发现目标智能体本身就是强大的下一步调用推测器，因此提出统一设计。

Method: 提出自推测智能体：单一模型在"智能体模式"下解决任务，在"推测器模式"下从部分轨迹预测下一步工具调用，完全复用前缀KV缓存。核心训练方法是**联合智能体-推测器强化学习（joint agent-speculator RL）**：从智能体自身 rollout 中推导推测目标，交替进行智能体和推测器的更新，避免性能退化。

Result: 在智能搜索问答和对话式工具使用任务上，Qwen3-4B 的平均下一步工具调用 Hit@1 从 44.1 提升至 61.2，Qwen3.5-4B 从 48.9 提升至 66.3，同时智能体任务成功率保持不变。

Conclusion: 统一智能体与推测器是可行且有效的设计，自推测方法能够在不牺牲任务性能的前提下显著减少工具调用等待延迟，为高效 LLM 智能体部署提供了简洁方案。

Abstract: Large language model agents often spend substantial wall-clock time waiting for tool call results. Tool-call speculation can hide this latency by predicting and pre-executing an agent's next tool call if the prediction matches the agent's eventual tool call, but existing speculators are typically separate draft models or cached traces that are poorly aligned with the deployed agent's own behavior. We identify this speculator-agent gap and show that the target agent itself is a strong next-call speculator. This points to a simpler design: unifying the agent and speculator within the same model. In this paper, we introduce the self-speculating agent, a single model that both solves tasks in agent mode and predicts its next tool call from partial trajectories in speculator mode, fully reusing prefix KV cache. To enable this dual-mode agent without degrading performance, we propose a joint agent-speculator reinforcement learning method, which derives speculation targets from the agent's own rollouts and alternates agent and speculator updates. Across agentic search QA and conversational tool-use agentic tasks, our method improves average next tool-call Hit@1 from 44.1 to 61.2 for Qwen3-4B and from 48.9 to 66.3 for Qwen3.5-4B, while preserving agent task success.

</details>


### [234] [Distributed Constraint Optimization via Online Learning and Iterative Pricing with Application to Large-Scale Satellite Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25835)
*Itai Zilberstein,Pranav Rajbhandari,Steve Chien,Tuomas Sandholm*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从两个互补方向解决大规模分布式约束优化问题(DCOP)：一是将DCOP与势博弈建立联系，采用在线学习算法进行均衡求解；二是提出迭代定价分解框架，将DCOP分解为任务分配的元级DCOP和局部调度优化子问题。两者结合在卫星调度实例上实现了99%以上的请求满足率，显著优于基线方法的87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中许多DCOP实例规模过大，无法进行整体求解。作者特别以大规模分布式卫星调度为驱动场景，需要有效的方法来处理大规模、通信受限的分布式决策问题。

Method: 方法包括两个互补部分：(1) 重新建立DCOP与势博弈(potential games)的联系，将现代在线学习算法适配到DCOP的均衡求解中；(2) 提出一种新的分解框架，将DCOP分离为两个交互子问题——用于任务分配的高层元DCOP(meta-DCOP)和用于调度的独立局部优化问题。通过一种新颖的迭代定价方法(iterative pricing)耦合两个层次，利用局部优化器的反馈来更新元级效用。

Result: 在线学习方法与代表性不完备DCOP算法相比具有竞争力。结合在线学习与迭代定价框架后，在真实世界的分布式卫星调度问题上实现了超过99%的观测请求满足率，而最先进的基线方法仅为87%。

Conclusion: 通过将基于势博弈的在线学习方法与迭代定价分解框架相结合，可以有效解决大规模DCOP问题，在真实卫星调度场景中取得了接近最优的性能，验证了方法的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Distributed constraint optimization problems (DCOPs) provide a popular framework for distributed decision making under limited communication, but many real-world instances are too large to solve monolithically. We address this challenge from two complementary directions. We revisit the connection between DCOPs and potential games, and adapt modern online learning algorithms for equilibrium finding to DCOPs. We show that these algorithms are competitive with representative incomplete DCOP algorithms. We then turn to decomposition frameworks for large-scale DCOPs, motivated by large-scale decentralized satellite scheduling. We propose a new framework that separates a DCOP into two interacting subproblems: a high-level meta-DCOP for task allocation, and independent local optimization problems for scheduling. To couple the two levels, we develop a novel iterative pricing method that updates the meta-level utilities using feedback from the local optimizers. Combining our online learning methods with our iterative pricing framework, we obtain near-optimal performance on real-world decentralized satellite scheduling problem instances, fulfilling over 99% of observation requests compared with 87% for state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [235] [HiSkill: Empowering LLM Agents with Hierarchical Skill Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25853)
*Yu Hao,Jinxuan Cai,Qi Zhang,Yawen Li,Zhiqiang Zhang,Chuan Shi,Cheng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HiSkill提出了一种层次化技能图框架，将LLM智能体的交互轨迹组织为包含技能节点、原子操作节点和类型化边的有向图，通过子图检索引导任务执行，在三个交互环境中超越现有方法并降低推理token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹到技能的方法通常产生扁平的高层文本技能集合，技能之间关系未被充分利用，且高层技能与可执行动作之间存在鸿沟。作者旨在通过层次化技能图来弥合这一差距，并充分利用技能间的结构关系。

Method: 提出HiSkill层次化技能图框架：(1)将交互轨迹组织为有向图，包含高层技能节点、AtomicOp（原子操作）节点及类型化边；(2)边类型涵盖分解、时序转换、兼容性、支持和恢复五种关系；(3)推理时检索任务相关子图，以符号化任务状态、活跃技能和子图共同引导LLM智能体迭代地进行技能切换、AtomicOp选择和可执行动作的具身化。

Result: 在三个交互环境上的实验表明，HiSkill优于所有当前最先进的基线方法，同时减少了推理过程中的token消耗。

Conclusion: 层次化技能图能够有效桥接高层技能与可执行动作具身化，技能关系的显式建模对提升LLM智能体在长周期交互任务中的表现至关重要。

Abstract: Skills have become an important abstraction for enabling large language model (LLM) agents to reuse past experience in long-horizon interactive tasks. However, existing trajectory-to-skill methods often produce flat collections of high-level textual skills that are stored and retrieved independently, leaving skill relations underutilized and maintaining a gap between high-level skills and executable actions. In this paper, we propose HiSkill, a hierarchical skill graph framework that organizes interaction trajectories into a directed graph with skill nodes, AtomicOp nodes, and typed edges. Specifically, the graph connects reusable high-level skills with executable action templates, while also capturing decomposition, temporal transition, compatibility, support, and recovery relations among them. At inference time, HiSkill retrieves a compact task-relevant subgraph and performs subgraph-guided task execution, where a symbolic task state, an active skill, and the retrieved subgraph guide the LLM agent to switch skills, select AtomicOps, and ground executable actions iteratively. Experiments on three interactive environments show that HiSkill outperforms state-of-the-art baselines while reducing inference token consumption, demonstrating the effectiveness of bridging high-level skills and executable action grounding through a hierarchical skill graph. Our data and code is available at https://github.com/BUPT-GAMMA/HiSkill.

</details>


### [236] [Runtime Uncertainty Monitoring for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems Using Bayesian Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25877)
*Bart Custers,Koorosh Aslansefat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM的多智能体框架用于精算风险建模，核心创新在于利用token级对数概率与贝叶斯网络进行不确定性传播，并通过长度归一化和校准将其转化为任务级置信度估计，在保持基线精算性能的同时提供了工作流稳定性与运行时不确定性的额外洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精算工作流属于高风险决策场景，LLM的概率本质及智能体间的依赖关系会引入不确定性，可能导致错误风险评估、不公平定价和监管违规，因此亟需一种能够量化并传播不确定性的框架。

Method: 提出一个中枢式多智能体框架，包含数据准备、建模、审查和解释等专业化智能体。核心方法为：提取token级对数概率，经长度归一化后汇总，再校准为任务级置信度估计，最后注入贝叶斯网络实现不确定性传播。

Result: 该框架能够复现基线精算性能，同时提供关于工作流稳定性和运行时不确定性传播的额外洞察。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的多智能体系统可以有效支持精算风险建模中的不确定性量化，所提出的不确定性传播方法在不牺牲精算准确性的前提下，增强了决策支持的可信度与透明度。

Abstract: This paper investigates how multi-agent systems (MAS)-based on large language models (LLMs) can support actuarial risk modelling, with a particular focus on uncertainty quantification. Actuarial workflows represent a high-stakes decision-support setting where unreliable outputs may lead to incorrect risk assessment, unfair pricing, and regulatory non-compliance. To address uncertainty introduced by the probabilistic nature of LLMs and dependencies between agents, a multi-agent framework is proposed in which specialised agents perform data preparation, modelling, review, and explanation tasks under a central hub. The main contribution is a novel approach to uncertainty propagation using token-level log-probabilities and a Bayesian Network. Importantly, log probabilities are not treated as direct probabilities of correctness or task success. Instead, length-normalised log-probability summaries are transformed into calibrated task-level confidence estimates before incorporation into the Bayesian Network. Results show that the framework reproduces baseline actuarial performance while providing additional insight into workflow stability and runtime uncertainty propagation.

</details>


### [237] [Distributing Security Controls Through Harness Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25890)
*William Robert Gore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究能否将现成的安全控制措施部署到商业AI编程智能体上，并通过自定义智能体框架（SHarD）分发给分布式用户。结果表明，SHarD在保持与直接安装安全控制的商业智能体同等效能的同时，实现了OS沙箱、技能扫描和工具限制三类安全控制的嵌入式分发，调整后得分达100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程智能体正被快速采用，但安全和风险问题仍是规模化推广智能体AI的主要障碍。现有的安全控制措施缺乏面向工程团队的系统化分发机制，且供应商原生方案引入的生态依赖未必适合所有部署场景。因此需要探索一种独立于供应商、可分布式部署的安全控制方案。

Method: 采用分阶段测试方法，在四种智能体配置（两款商业智能体分别带/不带安全控制、基准框架、安全加固框架SHarD）上，基于OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications构建的23项测试套件进行对比评估。SHarD基于Pi智能体框架构建，通过单条安装命令即可嵌入和分发OS沙箱、技能扫描和工具限制三类安全控制。

Result: SHarD获得100%的调整后得分，与最佳安全配置的商业智能体持平，且在所有测试类别中均未出现性能退化。还观察到模型非确定性会导致不一致的安全结果，以及自主智能体行为可能跨越系统边界——这正是OS沙箱可直接缓解的风险。初步提出了控制框架适配性评估框架的特征，并确定了第三个待研究问题。

Conclusion: 现成的安全控制措施可通过自定义框架有效嵌入并分发给分布式用户群，无需依赖供应商原生方案。SHarD证明了安全控制框架的可移植性与可扩展性，为组织在多样化部署环境中保障AI编程智能体安全提供了可行路径。

Abstract: AI coding agents are being adopted at historic speed, yet security and risk concerns remain the primary barrier to scaling agentic AI across organizations. Existing security controls for coding agents are not systematically distributed to engineering teams, and vendor-native solutions introduce ecosystem dependencies that may not suit every deployment context. This paper investigates whether off-the-shelf security controls can be implemented on commercial AI coding agents and scaled to a distributed user base via a custom agent harness. A phased testing methodology was applied across four agent configurations --- two commercial agents with and without controls, a baseline harness, and a security-hardened harness --- using a 23-test suite derived from the OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications. SHarD (Secure Harness Distribution), a distributable harness built on the Pi agent harness, demonstrated that three categories of security controls --- OS sandboxing, skill scanning, and tool restriction --- can be embedded and distributed via a single install command while retaining equivalent efficacy to direct installation on commercial agents. SHarD achieved an adjusted score of 100\%, matching the best securely configured commercial agent, with no regression across any test category. Notable observations include evidence that model non-determinism produces inconsistent security outcomes and that autonomous agent behavior can cross system boundaries in ways that OS sandboxing directly mitigates. Initial characteristics toward a control harness fitness framework are proposed, and a third research question is identified for future investigation.

</details>


### [238] [Messier: A High-Resolution Corpus for Cross-Benchmark Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25891)
*Stefan Krsteski,Charlotte Meyer,Guillaume Allegre,Tony O'Halloran,Alexandre Sallinen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Messier是一个包含957,253条记录的标准化语料库，统一了30个基准测试、714个智能体、11,891个任务和74,205个验证器的评估数据，揭示了AI智能体在不同基准类型上的进展不均衡，并提供了可复用的能力扩展基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体在交互式环境中的评估存在碎片化问题：任务、框架（scaffolds）、验证器和评分规则各自为政，导致不同基准测试的结果难以直接比较。已有工作要么局限于狭窄场景，要么规模不足，要么需要高昂的重新运行成本，使得大量实证记录缺乏可比性。

Method: 构建Messier语料库：整合公开基准分数，并在六个代表性不足的专业和科学领域（含一个法律基准）补充五智能体运行数据。每个记录按模型、框架、环境、任务、验证器和聚合规则进行标准化，并标注SOC/NAICS职业和行业分类。基于该语料库进行反事实重新评分分析，并从中推导能力量表，与Epoch的评估能力指数进行Spearman秩相关验证。

Result: 1）前沿进展在不同基准类型上不均衡："函数调用"已趋饱和，"编程"改进最快，"企业工作流"仍最具挑战性；2）反事实重新评分表明，多验证器任务中的严格全通过聚合会掩盖进展并人为扭曲智能体排名；3）推导出的能力量表与Epoch的评估能力指数达到Spearman ρ = 0.81的相关性，且可按领域、职业、动作空间或验证器类型进行专门化。

Conclusion: Messier为智能体能力扩展、基准审计和评估失败的细粒度分析提供了可复用的基础性基础设施，填补了跨基准标准化比较的空白。

Abstract: Evaluating AI agents in interactive environments is hindered by fragmented tasks, scaffolds, verifiers, and scoring rules. Existing efforts focus on narrow settings, remain limited in scale, or require costly reruns, leaving much of the empirical record incomparable. We introduce Messier, a unified corpus of 957,253 records that span 30 benchmarks, 714 agents, 11,891 tasks, and 74,205 verifiers. Messier consolidates public benchmark scores and supplements them with five-agent runs across six underrepresented professional and scientific domains, including a recent legal benchmark. Each record is standardized by model, scaffold, environment, task, verifier, and aggregation rule, with SOC/NAICS classifications for occupational and industry analysis. Using this corpus, we show frontier progress is uneven across benchmark types, with "function calling" saturated, "programming" improving the fastest, and "enterprise workflows" remaining the most challenging. Furthermore, counterfactual rescoring shows that strict all-pass aggregation in multi-verifier tasks can obscure progress and artificially alter agent rankings. From these standardized records, we derive capability scales that align with Epoch's Evaluation Capability Index rankings at Spearman \r{ho} = 0.81 and can be specialized by domain, occupation, action space, or verifier type. Messier provides a foundational, reusable infrastructure for agent capability scaling, benchmark auditing, and fine-grained analysis of evaluation failures.

</details>


### [239] [Interactive Reward Agent: GUI Task Evaluation via Environment-State Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25904)
*Chenrui Shi,Yuwei Wu,Yang Liu,Ruining Feng,Zirui Shang,Zhi Gao,Lifeng Fan,Che Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种交互式奖励智能体(IRA)，采用"先提议后验证"框架，通过访问GUI执行后的环境状态来可靠评估GUI任务完成情况。IRA在GUI-RewardBench上达到86.9%准确率，并成功用于GUI智能体的强化学习训练，在OSWorld上取得34.0%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GUI任务自动评估方法不可靠，因为它们仅依赖执行轨迹的截图，而许多任务完成判断需要访问环境状态（如系统配置、文件数据、应用设置等），这些信息无法从截图中获取。因此需要一种能获取和验证执行后环境证据的可靠评估方法，同时该评估结果可作为测试时扩展和训练后的奖励信号。

Method: 提出IRA（Interactive Reward Agent），基于"提议-然后-验证"（propose-then-verify）框架。给定任务指令和GUI智能体执行后的环境，IRA首先提出任务完成条件，然后通过调用三类工具（系统工具、应用工具和GUI工具）交互式地验证这些条件。该方法将可见界面证据与环境状态证据相结合，通过交互过程完成评估。

Result: 构建了GUI-RewardBench基准，涵盖321条GUI任务轨迹，横跨10个Ubuntu桌面应用类别。IRA在该基准上达到86.9%的准确率，优于现有评估器基线。将IRA应用于GUI智能体的强化学习训练，在OSWorld基准上取得了34.0%的成功率。

Conclusion: IRA通过交互式获取和验证环境证据，显著提升了GUI任务自动评估的可靠性。该方法不仅能准确评估任务完成情况，还能为GUI智能体的强化学习训练提供有效的奖励信号，证明了环境状态信息对GUI任务评估的关键作用。

Abstract: Graphical user interface task evaluation aims to determine whether a GUI agent has successfully completed a user instruction. Automated GUI task evaluation has received increasing attention because the evaluation results can serve as reward signals for both test-time scaling and post-training. However, reliable GUI task evaluation remains challenging because the judgments often require access to environment states, such as system configurations, file data, and application settings, beyond the screenshots of execution trajectories. In this paper, we propose an interactive reward agent (IRA) based on a propose-then-verify framework to acquire and verify evidence from the post-execution environment. Given a task instruction and a GUI environment after the GUI agent execution, IRA first proposes the task completion conditions and then verifies them by invoking system tools, application tools, and GUI tools. This design combines evidence from both visible interfaces and the environment state in an interactive process. We further introduce GUI-RewardBench, a benchmark of 321 GUI task trajectories spanning 10 Ubuntu desktop application categories. Experiments show that IRA achieves 86.9% accuracy on GUI-RewardBench, outperforming existing evaluator baselines. We further apply IRA to reinforcement learning of GUI agents, achieving a 34.0% OSWorld success rate, which demonstrates that IRA can provide effective reward signals for training GUI agents.

</details>


### [240] [Toward Standardized Cross-Vendor Agent Tool Trust Management in Autonomous Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25914)
*Ravi Kant Sharma,Ashutosh Uttam,Ajay Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentToolMO，一种面向3GPP NRM的智能体工具信任管理信息模型，通过形式化的信任状态机、阻尼级联传播、跨厂商信任通知及回溯影响评估，实现多厂商自主网络中的工具级信任可见性与近实时风险遏制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主网络L4-L5场景下，AI智能体需跨厂商调用工具，但现有管理标准缺乏跨厂商信任可见性机制。当Vendor B的工具被攻陷时，Vendor A的智能体因无法感知信任降级而持续调用，导致级联服务影响。此问题亟需标准化解决。

Method: 提出AgentToolMO信息模型，包含四大组件：(1)形式化定义的信任状态机，具备可证明的渐进式执行机制；(2)阻尼级联传播算法，保证有界收敛；(3)通过现有管理服务(MnS)接口实现的跨厂商信任通知；(4)基于NRM依赖图遍历的回溯影响评估。通过多厂商拓扑仿真验证模型有效性。

Result: 仿真评估表明：标准化跨厂商通知将爆炸半径从数小时级的未检测传播缩短至受MnS通知交付约束的近实时遏制；级联收敛在有限迭代内保证完成；跨厂商域的通知规模呈亚线性扩展。

Conclusion: AgentToolMO在现有3GPP管理基础设施内运行，利用既有协议，为可信的多厂商自主网络管理提供了标准化路径。

Abstract: Autonomous Network Levels 4-5 require AI agents to invoke tools across vendor boundaries without human oversight, yet existing management standards lack a standardized mechanism for cross-vendor trust visibility. When a tool from Vendor B is compromised, agents from Vendor A continue invoking it -- unaware of the trust degradation -- causing cascading service impact. We present AgentToolMO, a proposed 3GPP NRM information model for agent tool trust management. The model comprises: a formally defined trust state machine with provable graduated enforcement, damped cascade propagation with bounded convergence, cross-vendor trust notifications via existing Management Services (MnS) interfaces, and retroactive impact assessment through NRM dependency graph traversal. Simulation-based evaluation across multi-vendor topologies shows that standardized cross-vendor notifications reduce blast radius from hours-scale undetected propagation to near-real-time containment bounded by MnS notification delivery, with cascade convergence guaranteed in bounded iterations and sub-linear notification scaling across vendor domains. The framework operates within existing 3GPP management infrastructure, leverages existing protocols, and provides a standardization pathway for trustworthy multi-vendor autonomous network management.

</details>


### [241] [Penelope: Localized Latent Recurrence for Efficient Structured Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25915)
*Yutong Chen,Shouqian Shi,Xinran Liu,Haochen Wang,Jiaying Wang,Tianxing Xu,Yuanxi Wang,Zirui Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Penelope 是一种高效的隐式推理框架，在仅解码器 Transformer 的选定解码器区间内引入循环计算，通过时间调制的 GRU 动态迭代优化问题条件的边界记忆，无需生成冗长的显式推理轨迹即可在隐空间分配额外计算，实现了精度-效率的实用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型处理复杂结构化推理任务时，要么通过增大参数量（推高训练和部署成本），要么通过链式思维（CoT）序列化中间步骤（将推理计算与自回归输出长度绑定）。这两种方式均存在效率瓶颈，亟需一种能在不重复执行完整解码器、不生成长显式推理轨迹的前提下，灵活分配额外计算资源的机制。

Method: 提出 Penelope 框架，核心设计包括：(1) 将循环计算局部化到解码器的选定区间——下层解码器前缀仅执行一次以构建问题条件的边界记忆；(2) 通过时间调制的 GRU 动力学对该记忆进行迭代优化，产生循环读出状态；(3) 采用渐进式 CoT-to-latent 课程学习，将可见推理逐步迁移到内部循环路径中，最终在隐空间完成额外计算后再生成答案。

Result: 在开源结构化推理基准上，Penelope 在验证集选定的隐式计算预算下，取得了与现有隐式推理模型相比具有竞争力的准确率，同时显著降低了实测推理延迟。

Conclusion: 隐式优化可被局部化到窄解码器区间内，既能减少重复的全解码器执行，又避免生成长显式推理轨迹，为仅解码器 Transformer 模型提供了实用的精度-效率权衡方案。

Abstract: Complex structured reasoning tasks often require additional computation, yet current language models obtain it mainly by increasing parameter scale or by serializing intermediate steps as chain-of-thought (CoT) tokens. The former raises training and deployment costs, while the latter ties reasoning computation to autoregressive output length. We introduce Penelope, an efficient latent-reasoning framework for pretrained decoder-only Transformers that localizes recurrent computation to a selected decoder interval. The lower decoder prefix is evaluated once to construct a problem-conditioned boundary memory, which is then iteratively refined through time-modulated GRU dynamics and recurrent readout states before answer generation. A progressive CoT-to-latent curriculum transfers visible reasoning into this internal recurrent path, allowing additional computation to be allocated in latent space without repeatedly executing the complete decoder or generating a long intermediate trace. Experiments on open-source structured-reasoning benchmarks show that, at validation-selected latent budgets, Penelope attains competitive accuracy relative to established latent-reasoning models while reducing measured inference latency. These results show that latent refinement can be localized to a narrow decoder interval, reducing repeated full-decoder execution without generating a long visible reasoning trace and providing a practical accuracy-efficiency tradeoff for decoder-only Transformer models.

</details>


### [242] [dtControl2+$\varepsilon$: Trading Optimality for Explainability in MDPs via Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25925)
*Tereza Kinská,Jan Křetínský,Tobias Meggendorfer,Sabine Rieder,Maximilian Weininger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文扩展了dtControl2工具，引入"ε-近似"功能：在马尔可夫决策过程设置中，允许给定的不精确度ε≥0，构建更小的决策树来表示控制器策略，同时保证ε-最优性。该工具能生成比现有方法小几个数量级的决策树。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于大规模系统或存在大量边界情况的系统，即使采用dtControl2等先进工具，决策树表示仍然过于复杂，难以被人理解。直接缩减决策树规模又可能导致遗漏关键案例，使控制器出错。因此需要一种可控的简化方法，在可解释性与最优性之间取得平衡。

Method: 在马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）框架下，为dtControl2扩展"ε-近似"功能：给定允许的不精确度ε≥0，通过蒸馏控制器的核心逻辑，构建规模更小的决策树，同时严格保证策略的ε-最优性。该方法允许用户通过调节ε参数，可控地省略一定量的细节，实现可调节的简化解释。

Result: 该工具构建的决策树规模比现有最先进方法小几个数量级（orders of magnitude），同时保证了ε-最优性。

Conclusion: 通过在可解释性与最优性之间引入可调节的权衡机制（ε参数），该方法成功解决了大规模系统控制器表示过于复杂的问题，使得决策树表示在保持理论保证的前提下大幅精简，更易于人类理解。

Abstract: Over the past decade, decision trees have been used to represent controllers (a.k.a. policies) in an explainable way, with dtControl2 as a current state-of-the-art tool. However, for systems that are large or have many corner cases, even such representations tend to be too complex and not human-comprehensible. Unfortunately, reducing the size of the decision tree is not straightforward, as missing just a single crucial case might result in an incorrect controller. We tackle this issue in the setting of Markov decision processes, extending dtControl2 by "$\varepsilon$" functionality: Given an allowed imprecision $\varepsilon \geq 0$, we construct a smaller decision tree, distilling the essence of the controller, while still guaranteeing its $\varepsilon$-optimality. This enables us to provide tunably simpler explanations, omitting a controllable amount of detail. Our tool constructs decision trees that are orders of magnitude smaller than the state of the art.

</details>


### [243] [Large Language Model for Operations Research Formulation Selection in Multi-Warehouse Inventory Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25956)
*Jintao Xu,Yingzheng Ma,Jiong Dong,Yongzhi Qi,Jianshen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多仓库库存分配的混合整数规划（MIP）问题，提出一种求解器引导的大语言模型框架，将分配实例自动匹配到最优的OR专家公式。通过SFT、IPO和GRPO三阶段训练，在京东真实数据上显著提升了公式选择准确率和实际分配质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多仓库库存分配通常建模为MIP问题，但没有单一公式能在需求集中度、库存不平衡、补货规模、服务约束和预测波动等异构实例条件下始终表现最优。因此需要一种按实例动态选择最优OR公式的方法。

Method: 提出求解器引导的LLM框架，每个OR专家对应一种编码不同分配优先级的MIP公式。训练分三阶段：(1) 构建专家条件化的SFT数据进行模式学习；(2) 利用MIP求解器评估历史实例，将分配质量差距转化为边际加权的IPO偏好；(3) 生成每个实例的专家评分元数据，在GRPO阶段用作奖励查找表，对采样响应分配奖励。

Result: 在京东真实多仓库库存分配数据上，GRPO将Hit Ratio@1从21.45%提升至50.42%，Hit Ratio@2从70.47%提升至82.31%。最终选择器的分配准确率比现有基线提升12.57个百分点，与事后最优oracle的差距缩小至4.85个百分点。

Conclusion: 求解器引导的LLM框架能有效实现按实例的OR公式选择，GRPO阶段对选择精度的提升至关重要，该框架在实际电商库存分配场景中展现出显著优于固定公式和SFT+IPO选择器的分配质量。

Abstract: Multi-warehouse inventory allocation is typically formulated as a mixed-integer programming (MIP) problem, yet no single formulation consistently matches heterogeneous instance-level regimes induced by demand concentration, inventory imbalance, replenishment scale, service constraints, and forecast volatility. We study this issue as instance-wise operations research (OR) formulation selection, where each allocation instance is assigned to a solver-executable formulation from a candidate OR expert library. We propose a solver-guided large language model (LLM) framework for OR formulation selection, in which each OR expert corresponds to a MIP formulation encoding a distinct allocation priority. To train the selector, the framework first constructs balanced expert-conditioned supervised fine-tuning (SFT) records for schema learning, and then uses MIP solver evaluation on historical instances to convert solver-evaluated allocation-quality gaps into margin-weighted identity preference optimization (IPO) preferences and per-instance expert-score metadata for reward lookup during group relative policy optimization (GRPO) to assign rewards to sampled responses. Experiments on multi-warehouse inventory allocation instances from JD$\mathord{.}$com, one of China's largest e-retailers, demonstrate that GRPO substantially improves expert-selection accuracy relative to the SFT+IPO selector and, more importantly, produces higher realized allocation quality than both the preference-trained selector and the best fixed formulation. With GRPO, Hit Ratio@1 and Hit Ratio@2 increase from 21.45% to 50.42% and from 70.47% to 82.31%. The resulting selector achieves an allocation accuracy gain of 12.57 percentage points over the incumbent baseline, outperforming both the SFT+IPO selector and the best fixed OR expert, and reduces the gap to the ex-post oracle to 4.85 percentage points.

</details>


### [244] [CHARM: A Multimodal Graph Foundation Model with Hierarchical Context Modeling for Zero-Shot Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26023)
*Ankang Yang,Jitao Zhao,Di Jin,Yuxiao Huang,Dongxiao He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CHARM 是一种多模态图基础模型，通过层次化上下文建模实现零样本迁移。它将孤立节点替换为层次化图上下文以捕获多模态语义和跨模态关系，利用模态感知图上下文编码器与LLM集成，在零样本多模态图任务上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态图的零样本迁移研究不足：GNN类图基础模型需要下游适配，LLM类图方法多处理单模态或单域任务。核心挑战在于：(1) 模型需从各模态泛化知识并捕获可迁移的跨模态关系；(2) 缺乏目标域微调时，节点表示与域特定结构和模态特征纠缠，难以识别共享概念。

Method: 提出CHARM框架：(1) 用层次化图上下文替代孤立原始节点，捕获多模态语义和跨模态关系，将域特定节点模式映射到共享高层概念；(2) 设计模态感知图上下文编码器，融合多模态信息与图结构，将表示转化为图令牌；(3) 将图令牌输入大语言模型进行下游任务推理，实现无需目标域微调的零样本迁移。

Result: 在零样本多模态图任务上，CHARM相比基线方法取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: CHARM通过层次化上下文建模有效解决了多模态图零样本迁移中的跨模态关系捕获和域纠缠问题，为多模态图基础模型的发展提供了新方向。

Abstract: Graph foundation models (GFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for transferring knowledge across graph domains and tasks. Real-world graphs associate nodes with text, images, and other modalities, making multimodal graphs essential for representing complex entities and relations. Moreover, collecting labels and adapting models for every new graph domain is costly and often infeasible, motivating zero-shot transfer. Unfortunately, zero-shot transfer on multimodal graphs remains underexplored. Existing GNN-based graph foundation models typically require downstream adaptation, whereas LLM-based graph methods mainly address unimodal graphs or tasks within a single domain. This setting presents two key challenges. First, models must generalize knowledge from individual modalities while capturing transferable cross-modal relations. Second, without target-domain fine-tuning, node representations remain entangled with domain-specific structures and modality-specific characteristics, obscuring shared concepts in unseen domains. To address these challenges, we propose CHARM, a multimodal graph foundation model with hierarchical context modeling for zero-shot transfer. CHARM replaces isolated raw nodes with hierarchical graph contexts that capture multimodal semantics and cross-modal relations. These contexts map domain-specific node patterns to shared high-level concepts, reducing reliance on target-domain supervision or adaptation. A modality-aware graph context encoder integrates multimodal information with graph structure and converts the resulting representations into graph tokens for a large language model . Experiments show consistent improvements on zero-shot multimodal graph tasks.

</details>


### [245] [Falling Behind Drives Unsafe Development in an Idealised AI Race Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26034)
*Elias Fernández Domingos,The Anh Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过行为实验与演化模型，研究了技术竞赛中竞争压力如何促使参与者选择不安全的开发行为。结果表明，不安全行为更多源于竞赛中的策略状态（如落后追赶、对手行为模仿），而非个体的风险偏好，提示政策应聚焦于降低竞争压力与促进合作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI技术竞赛中，竞争压力被广泛认为会激励参与者采取更冒险、忽视安全的开发策略，即"速度-安全"张力。但现有讨论多基于理论推测，缺乏关于竞赛动态如何实际影响风险行为的实证证据。作者旨在通过受控实验，分离竞争结构不变条件下最大风险水平对行为的影响，并探究个体风险偏好与竞赛策略状态的相对作用。

Method: 采用框架化行为实验，配对参与者在不确定时间范围内重复选择"安全开发"或"不安全开发"。不安全开发带来更快进展和更高即时收益，但累积私人风险（上限分别为10%、60%、90%三种处理条件）；竞赛结构在所有处理中保持不变。除预注册的组间风险水平比较和风险偏好测量外，还进行了基于重复博弈结构的探索性分析，并构建了包含四种策略（Always Safe、Always Unsafe、Conditionally Safe、Conditionally Antisocial Safe）的简化演化模型来解释实验效应。

Result: （1）预注册假设未获支持：不同风险上限处理间，以及个体风险偏好对不安全选择的预测作用均不显著。（2）探索性分析发现：参与者更可能在对手选择不安全后跟随选择不安全；处于领先地位减少不安全行为，而落后则增加不安全行为；首轮选择可预测后续行为。（3）演化模型成功复现了处理效应，并展示了条件性不安全行为如何被竞赛动态所青睐。

Conclusion: 不安全开发行为主要由竞赛中的策略状态驱动——包括早期行为惯性、对手行为以及落后恐惧，而非单纯的个体风险偏好。因此，AI安全政策不应仅关注个体层面的风险认知，更应着力于降低竞争压力、遏制早期不安全行为的势头、以及促进开发者间的合作。

Abstract: Technological races create tension between speed and safety: actors may gain by moving faster than competitors, even when risky development is harmful. This is prominent in debates about artificial intelligence (AI), where competitive pressure is often argued to incentivise riskier, less safety-conscious development. We study this using a framed behavioural experiment based on an idealised AI race, in which paired participants repeatedly chose between Safe and Unsafe development under an uncertain time horizon. Unsafe development gave faster progress and higher immediate payoffs but accumulated private risk up to a treatment-specific maximum of 10\%, 60\%, or 90\%; the race's competitive structure was held constant, and only this maximum risk varied. Neither the pre-registered comparison between risk levels nor the role of elicited risk preferences was supported by the data. Instead, exploratory analyses motivated by the task's repeated structure show that Unsafe behaviour is shaped less by risk preferences than by the evolving strategic state of the race: participants are more likely to choose Unsafe after their opponent does so, being ahead reduces Unsafe play while falling behind increases it, and first-round choices predict later behaviour. To interpret these effects we introduce a reduced evolutionary model with four strategies -- Always Safe, Always Unsafe, Conditionally Safe, and Conditionally Antisocial Safe -- which reproduces the treatment effect and shows how conditional Unsafe behaviour can be favoured by competitive race dynamics. Together, the experiment and model show that unsafe development can emerge from early behavioural momentum, opponent behaviour, and fear of falling behind, rather than from risk preferences alone, suggesting policy should focus on reducing competitive pressure and promoting cooperation in AI development rather than only individual risk.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [246] [A Generalized Tangent Approximation based Variational Inference Framework for Strongly Super-Gaussian Likelihoods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.05431)
*Somjit Roy,Pritam Dey,Debdeep Pati,Bani K. Mallick*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于切变换的新型变分推理框架，针对具有强超高斯似然的概率模型，利用凸对偶构造对数似然的切下界函数，从而在高斯先验下诱导共轭性。该方法在温和的数据生成假设下提供了算法收敛保证和近极小极大最优的风险界，并在模拟与真实数据上展现出优于现有方法的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变分推理方法要么依赖刚性模型特定公式，要么依赖随机黑箱优化，而切近似方法虽利用概率模型几何结构，却长期局限于逻辑回归等少数模型。作者旨在将切近似方法推广到更广泛的强超高斯似然模型类别，克服黑箱方法缺乏理论保证的局限。

Method: 提出基于切变换的变分框架：利用凸对偶理论构造对数似然的切下界函数（tangent minorants），在原本难以处理的情况下诱导高斯先验与似然之间的共轭性；在温和数据生成机制假设下，建立算法收敛性保证，并推导变分风险的近极小极大最优界。

Result: 理论层面：建立了算法收敛性保证（区别于黑箱变分方法通常缺乏理论保证），推导了近极小极大最优的变分风险界。实验层面：在模拟和真实数据场景中，所提方法在可扩展性和稳定捕获复杂数据结构方面均优于当前最先进的变分算法。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将切近似变分方法从逻辑回归等有限模型推广至强超高斯似然这一大类概率模型，兼具理论保证和实际优越性能，为结构化变分推理提供了新的理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: Variational inference, as an alternative to Markov chain Monte Carlo sampling, has played a transformative role in enabling scalable computation for complex Bayesian models. Nevertheless, existing approaches often depend on either rigid model-specific formulations or stochastic black-box optimization routines. Tangent approximation is a principled class of structured variational methods that exploits the geometry of the underlying probability model. However, its utility has largely been confined to logistic regression and related modeling regimes. In this article, we propose a novel variational framework based on tangent transformation for a broad class of probability models characterized by strongly super-Gaussian likelihoods. Our method leverages convex duality to construct tangent minorants of the log-likelihood, thereby inducing conjugacy with Gaussian priors over model parameters in an otherwise intractable setup. Under mild assumptions on the data-generating mechanism, we establish algorithmic convergence guarantees, a contribution that stands in contrast to the limited theoretical assurances typically available for black-box variational methods. Additionally, we derive near-minimax optimal bounds for the variational risk. Superior performance of our proposed methodology is illustrated on simulated and real-data scenarios that challenge state-of-the-art variational algorithms in terms of scalability and their ability to consistently capture complex underlying data structure.

</details>


### [247] [Extended rank regression for all ordinal data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25006)
*Peter Hoff,Supratik Basu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩展秩伪似然的单调变换线性回归方法，无需估计均值-方差关系即可处理任意序数数据类型（连续或离散），通过Gibbs采样实现贝叶斯推断，并利用共形校准保证预测区间的边际频率覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回归模型中均值与方差关系的错误指定会严重影响推断准确性，但该关系通常并非研究兴趣所在，仅为讨厌参数。作者希望在不估计或预先指定该关系的前提下，构建一个能同时适应连续、离散序数数据以及各种均值-方差关系的统一回归框架。

Method: 核心方法基于扩展秩（extended rank）概念的伪似然，对单调变换线性回归模型进行推断。具体包括：(1) 利用秩的不变性消除单调变换参数，避免估计均值-方差关系；(2) 证明扩展秩似然在连续数据和二值数据两种极端情况下无渐近信息损失；(3) 通过Gibbs采样算法获得贝叶斯参数估计和预测区间；(4) 当模型存疑时，采用共形校准（conformal calibration）对贝叶斯预测分布进行校正，获得具有保证的边际频率覆盖的预测区间。

Result: 扩展秩似然在连续数据和二值数据两种极端情形下被证明无渐近信息损失；基于秩的预测区间能够在给定特征条件下获得近似覆盖控制；共形校准后预测区间具有保证的边际频率覆盖。

Conclusion: 所提出的扩展秩伪似然方法有效规避了均值-方差关系的估计难题，统一适用于各类序数数据，且推断效率在极端数据类型下不损失信息，结合共形校准进一步增强了模型的稳健性和实用性。

Abstract: The accuracy of inference from a regression model depends largely on how well the model represents the relationship between the mean and variance of the outcomes. As this relationship is rarely of direct interest, it is natural to treat it as a nuisance parameter, rather than attempt to estimate it. We take this approach in the context of a monotonically transformed linear regression model using a pseudo-likelihood based on an extended notion of ranks. This approach can accommodate a wide range of mean-variance relationships and any ordinal data type, including continuous and discrete ordered data, and requires no estimation or prior specification of the transformation, or decision to treat an outcome as continuous or discrete. We show that the extended rank likelihood incurs no asymptotic information loss at the two extremes of continuous and binary data, and that rank-based prediction intervals can obtain approximate coverage control conditional on the features. Bayesian parameter estimates and prediction intervals are available via a simple Gibbs sampling algorithm. For settings where the model is in doubt, conformal calibration of the Bayesian predictive distribution provides intervals with guaranteed marginal frequentist coverage.

</details>


### [248] [Simulation-based parameter estimation via a combination of embedded normalizing flows and implied empirical probabilities under moment restrictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25026)
*Getachew K. Befekadu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于仿真的参数估计框架，该框架通过嵌入式归一化流将复杂残差分布转换为简单基分布，并结合经验似然估计器在矩约束下进行参数推断，同时利用隐式微分获取梯度信息，最终得到可兼作代理模型的逆归一化流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当模型由物理系统的计算仿真定义时，传统参数估计方法面临残差分布复杂未知、难以直接施加约束的困难。本文旨在构建一个端到端的参数估计方案，既能处理复杂的残差分布形态，又能在分布层面对变换后的残差信息施加间接约束，从而实现对仿真模型参数的有效估计。

Method: 框架包含两个紧密集成的步骤：(1) 使用嵌入式归一化流（embedded normalizing flow）将未知复杂分布的残差信息变换到简单基分布；(2) 在矩约束下利用经验似然估计器对基分布施加间接约束，将变换后的残差视为来自有限离散分布的随机变量。参数更新采用一阶梯度方法，所有梯度信息通过经验似然函数的隐式微分获得。整个过程具有信息论解释，并给出了可算法化实现的计算框架。

Result: 该框架实现了对计算仿真模型参数的端到端估计。作为副产品，参数化嵌入式归一化流的逆映射（在估计参数值处）可作为原计算仿真模型的代理模型（surrogate model），能够用于模型差异量化和敏感性分析。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段框架——归一化流变换与经验似然约束相结合——为基于计算仿真的物理系统参数估计提供了一种有效且具有信息论基础的解决方案。通过隐式微分实现梯度计算使得整个流程可端到端优化，同时归一化流的逆映射作为代理模型进一步扩展了该方法的应用价值。

Abstract: In this work, we present a simulation-based parameter estimation framework for a model defined by a computational simulation of a physical system. We specifically outline an estimation framework consisting of two closely-integrated steps that facilitate an overall end-to-end parameter estimation scheme. The first step involves utilizing an embedded normalizing flow which is used to transform the unknown complex distribution of the residual information into a simple base distribution corresponding to the transformed residual information. In the second step, an empirical-likelihood estimator, under moment restrictions, is utilized for imposing an indirect constrain on the base distribution, where such an instantiated task reasonably allows us to treat the transformed residual information as random variables arising from discretely distribution population with each transformed data point as a single-cell from a set of finite-cell contingencies. Moreover, we use first-order gradient methods for updating the estimated parameter values of the model defined by the computational simulation and the corresponding parametrized embedded normalizing flow, that call for all gradient-related information by leveraging implicitly differentiations of the empirical-likelihood function, which is constructed from the implied empirical probabilities under moment restrictions. Here, it is worth mentioning that the problem formulation presented in this work, which highlights an information-theoretic interpretation, allows to present a computational framework for algorithmic implementations. Finally, as a-by-product, the inverse of the parametrized embedded normalizing flow, w.r.t. the estimated parameter values, serves as a surrogate model for the computational simulation model, which provides useful information for quantifying model discrepancies and sensitivity analysis.

</details>


### [249] [Handling Missingness and Censoring in Dirichlet Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25051)
*J. Pillay,A. Bekker,C. Tortora,A. Punzo*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对成分数据中存在缺失和截尾分量问题，在统一粗化框架下，提出了一种基于EM算法的Dirichlet分布参数极大似然估计方法，可同时实现参数估计与基于模型的插补，并在保持数据成分结构方面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于似然的成分数据推断通常要求完全观测，这限制了直接处理缺失或截尾分量。现有方法缺乏在统一框架下同时处理缺失与截尾数据、并保持单纯形上成分结构的能力。

Method: 提出统一的粗化框架（coarsening framework），在此框架下开发EM型算法，对Dirichlet分布参数进行极大似然估计。算法在保持成分数据结构的前提下，同时完成参数估计和基于模型的插补。通过模拟研究评估不同粗化机制下（含缺失和截尾）估计与插补的表现，并与现有基于模型的方法及非参数替代方法进行比较。在汞形态分析数据上进行实证验证。

Result: 模拟研究表明，所提方法在多种复杂粗化机制下表现良好。实证结果表明，Dirichlet分布适合汞形态分析数据，且其插补结果相比竞争方法更好地保留了观测数据的成分结构。

Conclusion: 所提出的EM算法在统一粗化框架下有效解决了成分数据中同时存在缺失和截尾的问题，在参数估计和保持成分结构的插补方面均优于现有方法，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Likelihood-based inference for compositional data generally requires fully observed compositions, hindering the direct treatment of missing or censored components on the simplex. In this paper, we develop an expectation-maximisation (EM)-type algorithm for maximum likelihood estimation of the Dirichlet parameters in the presence of missing and censored components under a unified coarsening framework. The Dirichlet distribution---the canonical probability model for compositional data, which plays a role analogous to that of the multivariate normal distribution for unconstrained multivariate data---provides the foundation for our methodology. Our methodology preserves the compositional structure of the data while simultaneously performing parameter estimation and model-based imputation. We evaluate the performance of our estimators and imputations through a simulation study under increasingly complex coarsening mechanisms, including both missing and censored data. We compare our method with an existing model-based approach and a nonparametric alternative. Finally, we illustrate the practical utility of our methodology using mercury speciation data, in which compositions are only partially observed because of detection limits and incomplete speciation. Our results indicate that the Dirichlet distribution provides a suitable model for these data and that our method yields imputations that better preserve the observed compositional structure than competing approaches.

</details>


### [250] [The positivity assumption in causal mediation analyses? Checked!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25072)
*Arthur Chatton,Geneviève Lefebvre,Mireille E. Schnitzer,Denis Talbot*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文将Positivity Regression Trees (PoRT)算法扩展至中介分析场景，用于检验受控直接效应、自然直接/间接效应及干预性中介效应的正性假设，并通过R包port提供实现工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理学研究中因果中介分析日益普及，但正性假设（positivity assumption）很少被提及和检验，原因在于：中介场景下的正性不仅需要对暴露变量和中介变量同时满足，且正性假设的具体形式取决于感兴趣的中介估计量，远比非中介的暴露效应分析复杂，缺乏相应的检验工具。

Method: 将PoRT算法（一种无需对建模或数据生成过程做假设的非中介正性检验方法）扩展至三种中介效应：受控直接效应（CDE）、自然直接/间接效应（NDE/NIE）以及干预性直接/间接效应（IDE/IIE）。通过R包port和GitHub上的Jupyter notebook提供实现。

Result: 通过心理健康领域的应用案例展示了该方法的实用性，并讨论了识别到正性违反时的后果及应对建议。

Conclusion: dePoRT扩展算法填补了中介分析中正性假设检验的工具空白，有助于心理学等领域的因果中介分析实践更加严谨。

Abstract: Causal mediation analyses are increasingly used in psychological sciences. Among the required assumptions, positivity is unfortunately seldom mentioned, likely due to the lack of tools for checking it. Mediational positivity is more complex than positivity in standard, non-mediated exposure effect analysis, because it requires positivity for both the exposure and the mediator, and because the specific form of the positivity assumption depends on the mediation estimand of interest. We propose an extension of the Positivity Regression Trees (PoRT) algorithm -- which was recently designed to check positivity in non-mediated settings without requiring assumptions about the modeling or the data-generating process -- to controlled, natural and interventional mediational effects. We illustrate its use through an application in mental health and have made it accessible through the port R package and a related notebook available at github.com/ArthurChatton/dePoRT-notebook. Finally, we discuss consequences and provide recommendations for when positivity violations are identified.

</details>


### [251] [Spectral Truncation in Synthetic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25074)
*Mojtaba Eslami*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究了谱合成控制法（Spectral SC），该方法在捐赠者面板的主导时序奇异向量定义的坐标中进行匹配，并提出了一种可分别调节保留与丢弃方向权重的混合估计量。作者证明了该估计族在满秩时退化为原始路径SC，并揭示了谱不平衡通过有限样本最佳线性预测分解映射为处理效应偏差的机制。通过大量蒙特卡洛模拟，发现截断谱SC在各类数据生成机制下均显著劣于调优后的原始路径SC，但预处理（去除单位和时间固定效应）后差距几乎消失，表明谱方法的适用性取决于基估计噪声、平衡欠定性及固定效应污染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统合成控制法在原始数据空间中匹配处理单元的处理前轨迹，但当数据存在噪声或高维特征时，直接匹配可能效率低下。本文旨在系统评估谱合成控制法——即在主导奇异向量张成的低维子空间中匹配——的统计性质与实际表现，并探讨其在何种条件下能够优于传统方法。

Method: 提出一个混合估计量框架：将谱合成控制分解为在保留的K个主导奇异向量方向上精确平衡，并允许在丢弃方向上独立调节权重，端点分别为原始路径SC和截断谱SC。理论分析包括：(1)满秩退化性质；(2)K维平衡在N0>K+1时的欠定性及仿射解集维度；(3)通过有限样本最佳线性预测分解将谱不平衡映射为处理效应偏差。评估采用11种数据生成机制、每种400次重复的蒙特卡洛模拟，利用仅捐赠者安慰剂检验选择正则化和混合权重。

Result: 截断谱SC在所有机制下的RMSE均显著高于调优后的原始路径SC，配对差异达4-11个蒙特卡洛标准误。混合估计量在多数重复中选择原始路径匹配，与调优SC在统计上无显著差异。结果对预处理高度敏感：使用原始输入时性能差距很大；而去除单位和时间固定效应后再做谱分解，差距几乎消失，且安慰剂检验开始偏向截断。这表明谱方法的有效性与基估计噪声、平衡欠定性和固定效应污染密切相关。

Conclusion: 谱合成控制法不应简单替代原始路径SC，而应被解释为一种诊断工具。其是否能够改善估计取决于三个关键因素：基估计的噪声水平、平衡条件的欠定性程度，以及固定效应是否已被适当去除。该研究为实践中何时采用谱方法提供了清晰的指导框架。

Abstract: Synthetic control (SC) matches a treated unit's pre-treatment trajectory to a weighted combination of donor units. We study Spectral SC, which instead matches the treated unit in coordinates defined by the leading temporal singular vectors of the donor panel, and a hybrid estimator that places separately tunable weight on retained and discarded directions, nesting raw-path SC and truncated Spectral SC as endpoints. We prove that the family reduces exactly to raw-path SC at full rank, that exact balance on $K$ retained dimensions with $N_0$ donors is underdetermined whenever $N_0>K+1$, with an affine solution set of dimension $N_0-K-1$, and that spectral imbalance maps to treatment-effect bias through a finite-sample best-linear-predictor decomposition. We evaluate the estimators across eleven data-generating regimes, using $400$ replications per regime and donor-only placebo validation to select regularization and the mixing weight. Truncated Spectral SC has significantly higher RMSE than tuned raw-path SC in every regime, with paired differences equal to $4$ to $11$ Monte Carlo standard errors. The hybrid estimator selects raw-path matching in most replications and is statistically indistinguishable from tuned SC in most regimes. The result is highly sensitive to preprocessing. With raw inputs, the performance gap is large; after removing unit and time fixed effects before spectral decomposition, as suggested by the assumptions behind our bound, the gap nearly disappears and placebo validation begins to favor truncation. We interpret these findings diagnostically rather than as evidence that Spectral SC should replace raw-path SC. Basis-estimation noise, balancing underdetermination, and fixed-effects contamination determine when spectral matching can help.

</details>


### [252] [Multilevel network meta-regression for multistate models: Population-adjusted joint synthesis of progression and survival data from individual and aggregate evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25120)
*Jeroen P Jansen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出多水平网络Meta回归-多状态模型（ML-NMR-MS），将ML-NMR的IPD+AgD整合框架嵌入疾病-死亡多状态模型，实现对PFS和OS的联合、人群调整的效应估计，解决了传统多状态NMA无法校正跨试验协变量不平衡的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肿瘤学时间-to-event结局的网络Meta分析中，多状态NMA（如Jansen等的方法）可联合合成PFS和OS，但无法校正效应修饰协变量在试验间的不平衡，导致相对效应估计可能有偏或不适用于目标人群。而ML-NMR虽能通过整合IPD和AgD解决协变量不平衡问题，却仅适用于单终点结局。因此需要一种能同时实现联合建模与人群调整的方法。

Method: 作者将ML-NMR的整合似然框架嵌入疾病-死亡多状态模型（稳定→进展→死亡）。对IPD试验，使用跨转换关联的完整多状态似然；对AgD试验，通过拟蒙特卡洛积分在重建的协变量分布上对转换强度进行积分，计算协变量边缘的状态占据概率，并代入条件生存似然。该模型可直接参数化状态转换经济模型。

Result: 在示例性肿瘤网络中，ML-NMR-MS能将治疗的整体效应分解为各成分转换（进展效应 vs. 进展后生存效应），并同时输出条件效应和边缘效应。模拟研究证实在正确设定下，该方法能无偏恢复真实效应，覆盖率接近名义水平。

Conclusion: ML-NMR-MS成功扩展了ML-NMR框架，实现了多状态模型下IPD与AgD的连贯整合，为肿瘤学NMA中PFS/OS的联合分析提供了人群调整的解决方案，兼具方法学严谨性与实际应用价值。

Abstract: In network meta-analysis (NMA) of oncology time-to-event outcomes, there is growing recognition that progression-free survival (PFS) and overall survival (OS) are better synthesized jointly than in separate analyses. The multistate NMA of Jansen et al. addresses this with a tri-state (stable, progression, death) Markov model fitted to aggregate survival curves, but it cannot adjust for imbalance in effect-modifying covariates across trials, so its relative effects may be biased or not relevant for the target population of interest. Multilevel network meta-regression (ML-NMR) resolves covariate imbalance by integrating an individual-level model over the aggregate covariate distribution, coherently combining individual participant data (IPD) and aggregate data (AgD); however, it has been developed only for single-endpoint outcomes. This paper introduces multilevel network meta-regression for multistate models (ML-NMR-MS), which embeds the ML-NMR integrated IPD-plus-AgD likelihood inside an illness--death multistate model. For IPD studies the full multistate likelihood over linked transitions is used; for AgD studies covariate-marginal state-occupancy probabilities are computed by quasi-Monte-Carlo integration of the transition intensities over the reconstructed covariate distribution, and entered through a conditional-survival likelihood. This yields population-adjusted joint PFS/OS treatment effects and can directly parameterize a state-transition economic model. On an illustrative oncology network, ML-NMR-MS resolves a treatment's joint effect into its component transitions, separating an effect on progression from an effect on post-progression survival, and produces conditional and marginal effects. A simulation study with fully known truth confirms unbiased recovery and near-nominal coverage under correct specification.

</details>


### [253] [Generalised Robust Bayes for Joint Inference of Model and Contamination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25665)
*Masahiro Fujisawa,Masaki Adachi,Takuo Matsubara*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: Hölder-Bayes 是一种基于广义贝叶斯推断的框架，利用 Hölder 散度同时推断模型参数和污染比例，提供定量的鲁棒性保证，并内置基于后验不确定性的异常检测机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒广义贝叶斯推断方法仅能定性降低后验对污染的敏感性，缺乏内在机制来量化污染比例或识别异常观测，亟需一个能够联合推断模型参数和污染程度并提供自包含异常检测的统一框架。

Method: 作者将 Hölder 散度应用于缩放后的模型密度，构建涵盖模型参数和污染参数的广义联合后验；理论上证明了后验影响函数的全局一致有界性（全局偏差鲁棒性）、有限样本超额风险界，以及在重污染机制下的 Bernstein-von Mises 近似和污染诱导偏差界；温度校准被解释为数据空间的仿射体积缩放。

Result: 理论层面建立了全局偏差鲁棒性、有限样本超额风险界及 BvM 近似；后验能同时输出模型参数和污染比例的不确定性，并传播至观测级别的检测频率得分，无需外部异常分数阈值即可实现异常检测。实验验证了鲁棒参数推断、污染水平恢复和不确定性感知的异常检测能力。

Conclusion: Hölder-Bayes 提供了一个定量鲁棒的广义贝叶斯框架，既能进行稳健推断，又能自包含地量化污染并检测异常，填补了现有 GBI 方法的空白。

Abstract: Generalised Bayesian inference (GBI) has emerged as a compelling robust alternative to standard Bayesian inference, mitigating sensitivity to data contamination by replacing the log-likelihood with a robust loss or divergence. However, existing robust GBI frameworks typically provide only qualitative robustness: while they can make posterior inference less sensitive to contamination, they lack an intrinsic mechanism to quantify the contamination proportion or identify anomalous observations. This paper introduces Hölder-Bayes, a GBI framework for joint inference of the model parameter and the contamination proportion. We construct a generalised joint posterior over both model and contamination parameter by applying the Hölder divergence to a scaled model density. Theoretically, we establish global bias-robustness via the uniform boundedness of the posterior influence function, derive a finite-sample excess-risk bound, and prove a Bernstein--von Mises approximation together with interpretable contamination-induced bias bounds under a heavy-contamination regime. We further show that, for the Hölder posterior, temperature calibration admits a direct interpretation as affine volume scaling of the data space. The resulting posterior yields a self-contained probabilistic mechanism for outlier detection: posterior uncertainty in both the model parameter and the contamination proportion is propagated to observation-level Frequency-of-Detection scores, without requiring an external anomaly-score threshold. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that Hölder-Bayes provides robust parameter inference, contamination-level recovery, and uncertainty-aware outlier detection.

</details>


### [254] [Covariate-Adaptive Sample Size Re-estimation for Population-Standardized Historical Control Designs in Single-Arm Trials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25159)
*Keisuke Hanada,Masahiro Kojima*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于外部对照单臂试验的群体标准化设计框架，通过倾向性评分将历史对照结局标准化至实际入组人群，并开发了一种结局盲态、协变量自适应的样本量重估（SSR）方法，仅利用基线协变量即可在入组过程中更新所需样本量，仿真表明该方法能维持目标检验效能，接近Oracle设计水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无法进行随机对照试验的情形下，外部对照单臂试验日益受到关注，但试验组与历史对照组之间的基线不平衡给效应估计和样本量规划带来困难。现有方法通常依赖事先的分布假设，当实际入组人群与预期存在分布偏移时功效不足，因此需要一种能适应实际入组人群特征、且保持结局盲态的样本量自适应策略。

Method: 提出群体标准化（population-standardized）设计框架：目标估计量针对实际入组的试验人群定义，通过预设的平衡性评分（如倾向性评分）对历史对照结局进行标准化。在此基础上，开发了结局盲态的协变量自适应SSR程序——结合初始基于情景的设计与序贯更新机制，仅用累积的基线协变量更新入组人群评分分布、标准化对照参数和目标样本量，并设定预设停止规则。给出了条件功效与无条件功效的解释，以及重复盲态SSR下近似I类错误控制的充分条件。

Result: 仿真研究表明，当计划的试验人群与真实入组人群存在分布偏移时，仅基于初始假设的固定设计功效下降，而所提SSR方法能将功效维持在目标水平附近，表现与Oracle设计相当。在基于ADCS的示例中，不同调整变量集下最终样本量有所不同，反映了入组人群协变量特征的动态变化。

Conclusion: 协变量自适应、结局盲态的样本量重估是一种实用的外部对照单臂试验设计策略，能够在保持盲态的前提下，针对群体标准化的治疗效应目标，适应入组人群特征的变化，维持目标统计效能。

Abstract: Externally controlled single-arm trials are increasingly considered when randomized controls are infeasible, but baseline imbalance between the active-arm trial and historical controls complicates both estimation and sample size planning. We propose a population-standardized design framework in which the target estimand is defined for the actually enrolled active-arm population and historical-control outcomes are standardized to that population through a pre-specified balancing score. Building on this estimand, we develop an outcome-blinded, covariate-adaptive sample size re-estimation (SSR) procedure that updates the required sample size using only accumulating baseline covariates, without using active-arm outcomes during enrollment. The method combines an initial scenario-based design with sequential updates of the enrolled-population score distribution, standardized control parameters, and target sample size under pre-specified stopping rules. We give conditional and unconditional power interpretations and sufficient conditions for approximate type I error control under repeated blinded SSR. In simulation studies with distributional shifts between planned and true active-arm populations, fixed designs based only on planning assumptions lost power, whereas the proposed SSR maintained power near the target level and performed similarly to an oracle design. In an illustrative ADCS-based example, the proposed procedures yielded different final sample sizes across adjustment sets, reflecting evolving enrolled-population covariate profiles. These results support covariate-adaptive, outcome-blinded SSR as a practical design strategy for externally controlled single-arm trials that target population-standardized treatment effects.

</details>


### [255] [Bias-corrected Cox regression with AI-extracted covariates via calibration summary statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25868)
*Arjun Sondhi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一个针对Cox比例风险模型的偏差校正框架，用于解决AI从临床记录中提取协变量时引入的测量误差问题。该方法将偏差分解为校准项与高阶残差，提供了一种事后矩阵乘法的校正估计器及带有校准不确定性的置信区间，并给出了数据供应商应提供的汇总统计量规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模观察性研究越来越多地依赖AI从非结构化临床记录中提取结构化变量，但数据供应商仅提供提取数据和汇总准确率统计量，下游研究者无法直接评估提取误差对生存分析推断的影响。现有方法缺乏针对Cox模型下AI提取误差的校正框架。

Method: 在统一的多变量校准框架下，将Cox模型下朴素估计量的偏差分解为：一个主导的一阶校准项和一个随提取精度提高而消失的二阶残差。基于主导项构造校正估计器，可通过标准Cox软件输出的后验矩阵乘法实现。进一步推导了融合校准不确定性的偏差调整置信区间，并提出敏感性诊断工具以评估被忽略残差对推断的实质影响。

Result: 合成数据实验表明，在交叉依赖提取误差和受控非线性校准违反条件下，所提校正方法能显著降低偏差并实现接近标称水平的覆盖率，即使在线性校准假设存在轻微违反时依然稳健。

Conclusion: 该框架为数据供应商提供了具体的报告规范——在提供任何AI提取的协变量数据集时，应附带一组简洁的汇总统计量，以支持生存分析中的偏差校正和推断可靠性评估。

Abstract: Large-scale observational studies increasingly rely on AI pipelines to extract structured variables from unstructured clinical records. A common workflow separates the data vendor, who validates extraction accuracy with a gold-standard sample, from the downstream researcher, who receives only the extracted dataset and summary accuracy statistics. We develop a bias-correction framework for the Cox proportional hazards model when covariates are subject to AI extraction error. Within a unified multivariate calibration framework, we show that the naive Cox estimator's bias decomposes into a leading-order calibration term and a second-order residual that vanishes as extraction accuracy improves. The leading-order term yields a corrected estimator that operates as a post-hoc matrix multiplication on the output of any standard Cox software. We further derive bias-adjusted confidence intervals that incorporate calibration uncertainty and a sensitivity diagnostic for assessing whether the neglected residual could materially affect inference. Synthetic data experiments with cross-dependent extraction errors and controlled nonlinear calibration violations confirm that the correction substantially reduces bias and achieves near-nominal coverage even under mild violations of the linear calibration assumption. The framework yields a concrete reporting specification: a short list of summary statistics that data vendors should provide alongside any AI-extracted covariate dataset used in survival analysis.

</details>


### [256] [Differential Equation-Constrained Exponential-Type Local Polynomial Regression Under Model Misspecification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25248)
*Chunlei Ge,W. John Braun*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受微分方程约束的回归框架，利用一阶微分方程并适配局部多项式回归技术。重点研究局部指数增长模型，分析不同阶数Taylor多项式核估计的渐近偏差与方差，并通过模拟研究评估模型在误设场景下的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型误设是应用统计建模中常见但难以避免的关键问题。作者旨在通过引入微分方程约束来强化模型结构，从而缓解模型误设带来的影响，提高统计推断的可靠性。

Method: 提出一种微分方程约束的回归框架：1）利用一阶微分方程作为结构约束；2）结合局部多项式回归技术进行非参数估计；3）针对局部指数增长模型（指数型微分方程），推导不同阶数Taylor多项式核估计量的渐近偏差与渐近方差；4）设计两种不同程度的模型误设场景进行模拟比较研究。

Result: 推导出局部指数增长模型下，基于不同阶Taylor多项式的核估计量的渐近偏差和方差表达式。模拟研究表明，在不同误设水平下，各估计量表现出不同的稳健性特征。

Conclusion: 通过微分方程约束强化模型结构是缓解模型误设问题的有效途径。局部多项式回归技术与微分方程约束的结合，为处理模型误设提供了新的统计建模框架。

Abstract: The issue of model misspecification is critical, yet it is often regarded as unavoidable in applied statistical modeling. Model misspecification can be mitigated by incorporating informative features and strengthening model formulations, such as through the integration of domain knowledge or structural constraints. In this paper, we propose a regression framework constrained by differential equations, which leverages first-order differential equations and adapts local polynomial regression techniques. Specifically, we focus on the local exponential growth model, characterized by an exponential-type differential equation. For this model, we examine the asymptotic biases and variances of kernel estimators constructed using Taylor polynomials of varying degrees. To evaluate model robustness, we conduct simulation studies comparing different estimators under two misspecification scenarios varying the levels of misspecification.

</details>


### [257] [A Copula-Based Regression Framework for Enhanced Prediction under Heteroscedasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25250)
*Deepani Hemachandra,Jagath Senarathne,Mahasen Dehideniya*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Copula的回归框架，用于建模具有异方差误差结构的数据，无需严格的分布假设。仿真和真实数据结果表明，该方法在预测精度上显著优于传统线性模型和对数线性模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典回归方法（如普通最小二乘法）依赖同方差和残差正态性等强假设，这些假设在真实数据中常被违背。对数变换虽常用于稳定方差，但可能引入反变换偏差，且无法充分解决异方差和不对称依赖结构问题。

Method: 提出基于Copula的回归框架，通过Copula函数显式建模异方差误差结构，不依赖限制性分布假设。通过综合仿真研究（在不同依赖水平和异方差场景下）以及ISLR包中Wages数据集的真实数据应用进行验证。

Result: 仿真中Copula模型平均MAPE为0.21，优于线性模型(0.27)和对数线性模型(0.36)；预测误差相对线性模型降低约6%-33%，相对对数线性模型降低24%-57%。真实数据应用中，传统对数线性模型未能充分捕捉异方差，而Copula回归框架给出了更准确的预测。

Conclusion: Copula回归在存在异方差和复杂依赖结构的情况下是一种有效的建模替代方案，具有良好的适用性和预测精度。

Abstract: Classical regression approaches, including ordinary least squares, rely on strong assumptions such as constant variance and normality of residuals, which are often violated in real-world data. Although log-transformation is commonly used to stabilise variance, it may introduce re-transformation bias and fail to address heteroscedasticity and asymmetric dependence structures adequately. To overcome these limitations, this study proposes a copula-based regression framework for modelling data in the presence of heteroscedastic error structures. The proposed approach explicitly accounts for heteroscedasticity without requiring restrictive distributional assumptions. A comprehensive simulation study is conducted under varying dependence levels and heteroscedastic scenarios to compare the performance of the proposed method with usual regression models and log-linear models. The simulation results demonstrated that the proposed copula-based model consistently outperformed conventional approaches, achieving an average mean absolute percentage error of 0.21, compared with 0.27 and 0.36 for the linear and log-linear models, respectively. Across all simulation settings, the copula-based model reduced prediction errors by approximately 6%-33% relative to the linear model and 24%-57% relative to the log-linear model. A real-data application using the Wages dataset from the ISLR package in R further confirmed these findings, where conventional log-linear models failed to adequately capture heteroscedasticity. In contrast, the proposed copula-based regression framework produced more accurate predictions, demonstrating its effectiveness for modelling heteroscedastic data. Overall, the results demonstrate that copula-based regression represents a viable modelling alternative in the presence of heteroscedasticity and complex dependence structures.

</details>


### [258] [Penalized likelihood inference for beta-binomial meta-analysis of proportions of rare events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25320)
*Kotaro Sasaki,Hisashi Noma*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对稀有事件比例的Meta分析中，普通最大似然估计存在有限样本偏倚问题，本文提出基于Jeffreys先验惩罚的最大惩罚似然估计方法，并引入剖面惩罚似然置信区间，模拟研究表明该方法在收敛率、偏倚和均方根误差方面优于普通MLE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在比例Meta分析中，稀有事件导致零事件研究频发、数据稀疏。Beta-binomial模型虽能灵活处理过度离散和稀有事件比例，但常用的最大似然估计（MLE）在样本量小或事件概率接近边界时存在明显的有限样本偏倚，需要更稳健的估计方法。

Method: 提出最大惩罚似然估计（MPLE），通过对数似然函数加上Jeffreys先验惩罚项进行最大化。同时引入剖面惩罚似然置信区间（profile penalized likelihood CI）作为一种在稀疏数据下更可靠的推断方法，并与传统Wald型置信区间进行比较。

Result: 模拟研究表明，所提估计量在研究数量较少或事件概率较低的场景下，在收敛率、偏倚和均方根误差方面均优于普通MLE。剖面惩罚似然置信区间能有效维持接近名义水平的覆盖率。

Conclusion: 所提出的最大惩罚似然方法为稀有事件比例Meta分析提供了一种优于普通MLE的有效替代方案，尤其适用于研究数少或事件概率低的情形。

Abstract: In meta-analyses of proportions, the event of interest is often rare, resulting in sparse event counts and frequent zero-event studies. The beta-binomial model has been used as a flexible random-effects model for pooling overdispersed and rare-event proportions. However, the commonly used maximum likelihood estimator (MLE) may be subject to finite-sample bias, which may be particularly relevant when the number of studies is small or the mean event probability is close to the boundary. In this article, we propose a maximum penalized likelihood estimator for the beta-binomial random-effects model. The proposed estimator is obtained by maximizing the log-likelihood augmented by the Jeffreys-prior penalty, which has shown favorable finite-sample bias and stability properties in a range of sparse-data models. In addition to Wald-type confidence intervals (CIs), we introduce profile penalized likelihood CIs as a potentially more reliable approach to inference in sparse-data settings. A simulation study showed that the proposed estimator generally outperformed the ordinary MLE, particularly in settings with fewer studies or lower event probabilities, in terms of convergence rate, bias, and root mean squared error. Additionally, the profile penalized likelihood CIs effectively maintained coverage close to the nominal level. The proposed method provides a useful alternative to ordinary MLE for meta-analyses of rare-event proportions.

</details>


### [259] [On the magnitude, sign and ranking of recanting-twin path-specific effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25709)
*Tran Trong Khoi Le,Pham Hien Trang Tu,Nhat Long Ngo,Tat-Thang Vo*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文系统刻画了recanting-twin路径特异性效应（PSEs）在量级、符号和排序上的行为，以自然PSEs为基准。作者推导了二元结局下两类效应绝对差异的非参数可识别上下界，并通过模拟研究发现，在非线性、非单调场景中，符号不一致比例为5%-43%，排序不一致比例为15%-56%，且不一致性受不可观测的中介混杂因子反事实相关性强烈影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: recanting-twin PSEs框架虽能解决因果中介分析中的中间混杂问题，并实现平均处理效应的细粒度路径特异性分解，但尚不清楚这些效应在方向和相对量级上多大程度反映自然PSEs。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 首先，在二元结局设置下，推导recanting-twin PSEs与自然PSEs之间绝对差异的非参数可识别上下界；其次，通过模拟研究，在四种场景、320个数值设置中评估两类效应在符号和排序上的不一致频率，并考察不可观测的中介混杂因子反事实相关性的影响。

Result: （1）推导的上下界为评估两类效应量级差异是否显著提供了实用工具；（2）排序不一致比例为15%-56%，符号不一致比例为5%-43%；（3）不一致性受中介混杂因子反事实值之间不可观测相关性的强烈影响。

Conclusion: 在解读recanting-twin PSEs的量级和排序时需保持谨慎；未来研究应识别在非单调性存在时两类PSEs仍能得出一致结论的条件。

Abstract: The framework of recanting twin path-specific effects has recently been propose to address the issue of intermediate confounding in causal mediation analysis, enabling the decomposition of the average treatment effect into identifiable fine-grained path-specific effects (PSEs). An open question, however, is the extent to which recanting-twin PSEs reflect the direction and relative magnitude of their corresponding natural PSEs. In this paper, we systematically characterize the behaviors of the recanting-twin PSEs in terms of magnitude, sign and ranking, benchmarking against natural PSEs. To achieve this, we first derive non-parametric, identifiable upper and lower bounds for the absolute difference between recanting-twin and natural PSEs in binary outcome settings. These bounds provide a practical way to assess whether discrepancies in magnitude between the two types of effects are substantial, thereby facilitating the use of recanting-twin PSEs as informative approximations of their natural counterparts. A simulation study is then conducted to evaluate frequency of disagreements in sign and ranking between recanting-twin and natural effects in non-linear, non-monotonic scenarios. Across four scenarios and 320 numerical settings, we find that disagreement ranges from 15% to 56% for ranking and from 5% to 43% for sign. Disagreement is strongly influenced by the non-observable correlation between counterfactual values of the intermediate confounder, suggesting that caution is needed when interpreting the magnitude and ranking of recanting-twin PSEs, and motivating future work to identify conditions under which the two types of PSEs yield consistent conclusions when non-monotonicity presents.

</details>


### [260] [Nonparametric Bayesian inference for the Gini-Simpson index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25737)
*Pier Giovanni Bissiri,Riccardo Corradin,Andrea Ongaro*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文比较了多种用于物种频率建模的非参数先验分布，指出现有对称Dirichlet分布在期望和离散度方面存在缺陷，并提出一种参数依赖于物种数量的改进对称Dirichlet设定；同时在无限物种情形下，Poisson-Dirichlet模型优于经典的Ferguson Dirichlet过程，其后验均值可表示为最优经典无偏估计量与先验期望的凸组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物种多样性评估是理解种群结构的关键步骤，Gini-Simpson指数是最广泛使用的多样性度量之一。然而，现有的非参数先验模型（如对称Dirichlet分布、Ferguson Dirichlet过程）在期望、离散度等统计性质上存在不令人满意的表现，亟需更合理的先验设定来改进物种频率建模。

Method: 本文在非参数贝叶斯框架下，系统比较了多种已有的物种频率先验分布；提出一种改进的对称Dirichlet分布，其参数依赖于物种数量，具有良好的解析可处理性和可解释性；在无限物种设定下，引入Poisson-Dirichlet模型替代Ferguson Dirichlet过程，并推导了多样性指数后验均值的凸组合表示；同时辅以渐近分析进行理论支撑。

Result: （1）传统对称Dirichlet分布在期望和离散度方面存在不良性质，改进的参数依赖物种数的对称Dirichlet设定可缓解这些问题；（2）在无限物种情形下，Ferguson Dirichlet过程表现不佳，而Poisson-Dirichlet模型更优；（3）Poisson-Dirichlet模型下，Gini-Simpson多样性指数的后验均值可分解为最优经典无偏估计量与先验期望的凸组合；（4）渐近分析进一步支持了上述理论结果。

Conclusion: 在物种多样性建模中，应优先采用参数依赖物种数的对称Dirichlet先验以克服传统设定的缺陷；对于无限物种场景，Poisson-Dirichlet过程是比Ferguson Dirichlet过程更合理的选择，其后验推断具有优美的凸组合结构，兼具经典频率学派估计与贝叶斯先验信息的优点。

Abstract: Many statistical problems concern the analysis of species distributions or, more generally, of discrete labeled quantities. Assessing species diversity constitutes a key step toward understanding population structure, and the Gini-Simpson index is among the most widely adopted diversity measures. In this manuscript, we examine several well-established nonparametric prior models for species frequencies and compare them with a newly proposed distribution within this framework. Specifically, we demonstrate that the conventional symmetric Dirichlet distribution leads to certain undesirable properties in terms of expectation and dispersion. These limitations can be mitigated by adopting an alternative symmetric Dirichlet specification, in which the parameter depends on the number of species. This modified formulation is characterized by analytical tractability and interpretability of its main summaries. Furthermore, when the number of distinct species is infinite, the classical Ferguson Dirichlet process exhibits unsatisfactory behavior compared to the more general Poisson-Dirichlet model. Notably, within this latter model, the posterior mean of the diversity index can be expressed as a convex combination of the optimal classical unbiased estimator and the prior expectation. Theoretical results are further supported by asymptotic analyses and systematically compared with their classical counterparts.

</details>


### [261] [Calculating the Expected Value of Sample Information accounting for missing data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25775)
*Luca Benetti,Gianluca Baio,Anna Heath*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在存在缺失数据的情况下计算样本信息期望值（EVSI）的方法，通过模拟MCAR、MAR和MNAR三种缺失机制并结合多重插补与非参数回归，发现缺失数据会降低EVSI，且恢复理想EVSI所需样本量超过传统方法，为现实研究中的样本量计算提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EVSI传统上仅应用于理想化的数据采集机制，未考虑真实研究中普遍存在的缺失数据问题，这极大限制了其在现实卫生经济学评估中的应用潜力。

Method: 首先模拟个体层面数据并引入MCAR、MAR、MNAR三种缺失机制；接着采用多重插补方法校正缺失数据带来的偏差；最后基于插补后的数据，利用非参数回归方法计算EVSI。在两个卫生经济学模型上对比无缺失与不同缺失类型下的EVSI，并定义恢复理想EVSI所需额外样本量的高效计算方法。

Result: 缺失数据会导致EVSI下降；为弥补缺失数据影响所需额外采集的样本量超过传统标准方法给出的估计值。

Conclusion: 该方法将EVSI计算拓展至MAR和MNAR等非平凡缺失场景，使其更贴近现实研究条件。恢复理想EVSI所需样本量大于传统方法的结果表明，该框架有潜力成为现实研究中样本量计算的新技术。

Abstract: The Expected Value of Sample Information (EVSI) is a powerful instrument to determine the value of additional evidence to inform an economic model. However, EVSI has been applied only to idealized data collection mechanisms, thereby reducing its potential applications in realistic studies. In this paper, we define a methodology to calculate EVSI when the additional evidence we aim to collect exhibits missing data; a very common challenge in real-world studies. First, we define how to simulate individual-level data and how to induce missingness inside the simulated data. We will reproduce Missing Completely At Random (MCAR), Missing At Random (MAR), and Missing Not At Random (MNAR) missing data mechanisms. Then, we will apply the multiple imputation method to adjust for the bias related to the missing data. Finally, we use the imputed data to compute the EVSI using nonparametric regression methods. We apply the novel methodology to two different health economic models and compare the EVSI computed on data without missingness with the EVSI with different types of missing data. We show that the EVSI decreases when the additional evidence suffers from missingness, and therefore, we define a method to efficiently compute the sample size we need to collect to recover the idealized EVSI (without missingness). We find out that the number of additional samples needed to correct for missing data exceeds that coming from standard approaches. With this methodology, we compute EVSI when the additional data are affected by non-trivial forms of missingness, modeling both the MAR and MNAR mechanisms, and extending EVSI calculation to more realistic scenarios. The fact that the necessary sample size to recover the idealized EVSI exceeds that of standard methods suggests that this methodology potentially represents a novel technique for sample size calculation in realistic studies.

</details>


### [262] [Causally Interpretable Meta-Mediation Analysis With Missing At Random Mediator and Outcome Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25822)
*Marie-Félicia Beclin,Apolline Courrèges-Vartanian,Geneviève Lefebvre,Tat-Thang Vo*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的中介荟萃分析方法，通过将各研究的自然间接效应估计传输到明确界定的目标人群后再进行证据合成，解决了传统结构方程模型方法无法处理中介-结局混杂、缺失数据及目标人群不明确等问题，并开发了数据自适应估计量和异质性分解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的中介荟萃分析基于结构方程建模，存在三个核心局限：(1) 无法处理中介-结局混杂；(2) 难以扩展以应对缺失的中介和结局数据；(3) 汇总间接效应的目标人群不清晰。此外，现有方法无法有效整合那些虽未明确研究中介但收集了中介数据的独立研究。

Method: 基于半参理论，作者提出了一种传输方法：先将各研究的自然间接效应估计传输（transport）至预先明确界定的目标人群，再进行证据合成。构建了灵活的数据自适应估计量。同时开发了新型随机效应模型和基于ANOVA平方和的非参数类比方法，将研究间异质性分解为影响因果可解释性的不同来源。

Result: 通过模拟数据和真实数据评估了所提方法的有限样本性能，验证了该方法在多种场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法有效克服了传统中介荟萃分析的局限性，能够提供更具因果可解释性的汇总间接效应估计，并可整合更广泛的证据来源。

Abstract: Meta-analyzing natural indirect effect estimates from multiple studies is increas- ingly used to synthesize evidence on causal pathways of interest. However, stan- dard mediation meta-analysis approaches are typically based on structural equation modeling, which fails to account for mediator-outcome confounding, is not read- ily extended to address missing mediator and outcome data, and is often unclear about the target population to which the summary indirect effect pertains. In this work, we propose a novel method that addresses these limitations. Our ap- proach transports study-specific natural indirect effect estimates to a well-defined target population prior to evidence synthesis. The proposed methods enable the integration of studies that do not explicitly investigate mediation but collect data on the mediator to improve extensiveness. Using semiparametric theory, we con- struct flexible, data-adaptive estimators for the target parameter. Novel random- effects models and non-parametric analogues based on ANOVA sums of squares are also developed to decompose between-study heterogeneity into distinct sources that may affect the causal interpretability of the obtained findings. Finite-sample per- formance of the proposed methods is evaluated through simulated and real-world data.

</details>


### [263] [Testing Microbiome Community Differences in High Dimensions: A Bootstrap Approach for Compositional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26022)
*Monika Bhattacharjee,Nilanjan Chakraborty,Sayan Das,Sounak Chakraborty,Lei Liu,Yiming Shi,Kristine M. Wylie,Todd N. Wylie,Molly J. Stout*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于经验自助法的假设检验框架，用于比较不同组别间微生物群落均值的差异，适用于两样本、配对及多样本场景。该方法充分考虑了微生物组数据的组合性（单纯形结构），在高维情形下仍能提供有效推断，并在结直肠腺瘤/癌和早产妊娠两项大规模研究中验证了其识别临床意义差异的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微生物组数据具有组合性（compositional），即相对丰度数据受限于单纯形空间，违反了许多经典统计推断方法（如t检验、ANOVA）的基本假设。现有方法要么忽视组合性导致假阳性升高，要么在微生物种类数远大于样本数的高维情形下失效，亟需一种既能尊重数据组合结构又能在高维下稳健工作的假设检验框架。

Method: 提出基于经验自助法（empirical bootstrap）的假设检验框架：首先在单纯形空间中对微生物组数据进行适当的中心化变换（如中心对数比变换 clr），然后利用重抽样策略构建检验统计量的零分布，从而在无需依赖渐近正态性假设的情况下进行组间均值相等性检验。该方法天然适配两样本、配对及多组比较场景，且在高维（p >> n）情形下仍能保持有效的第I类错误控制。

Result: 在两项大规模实证研究中：①结直肠腺瘤与癌患者的粪便微生物组数据中，发现了与年龄相关的腺瘤患病率差异；②妊娠结局（早产与否）的阴道微生物组数据中，揭示了与种族相关的阴道微生物群落组成差异。上述差异在传统方法中均未被检测到，验证了所提方法在挖掘临床相关微生物标志物方面的优势。

Conclusion: 基于重抽样的推断方法能够有效应对微生物组数据的组合性约束和高维挑战，识别出传统方法遗漏的临床重要差异。该框架有望提升微生物组研究的可重复性，推动临床相关微生物特征标志物的发现。

Abstract: Understanding differences in microbial community structure is critical for uncovering risk factors and mechanisms underlying diseases such as colorectal cancer and preterm birth. Microbiome data present unique statistical challenges because they are compositional in nature, violating assumptions of many classical inference procedures. We propose an empirical bootstrap framework that enables robust hypothesis testing for equality of microbial community means across groups, including two-sample, paired, and multi-sample settings. The method accounts for the simplex structure of microbiome data and provides valid inference even in high-dimensional regimes. Through applications to two large-scale studies, fecal microbiota in colorectal adenoma and cancer patients, and vaginal microbiota in pregnancy with preterm birth outcomes-we demonstrate that our approach identifies clinically meaningful differences that conventional methods fail to detect, such as age-related differences in adenoma prevalence and race-associated disparities in vaginal microbiome composition. These results highlight the potential of resampling-based inference for advancing microbiome research, improving reproducibility, and uncovering clinically relevant microbial signatures.

</details>


### [264] [Probabilistic Symbolic Regression for Equation Discovery via Operator-induced and Regularized Symbolic Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.19710)
*Somjit Roy,Pritam Dey,Bani K. Mallick,Debdeep Pati*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种概率符号回归框架，将数学表达式表示为符号树的集成，通过正则化先验控制表达式复杂度，利用Occam窗后验摘要捕捉符号不确定性，并提供了后验集中性理论保证，在基准科学方程和材料发现问题中展现出优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号回归方法依赖启发式搜索，在噪声环境下难以平衡预测精度与表达式复杂度，且对符号不确定性的刻画有限。统一解决这些问题的概率方法尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出概率符号回归框架：(1)将数学表达式建模为符号树的集成；(2)引入正则化先验控制树拓扑结构的复杂度；(3)采用基于Occam窗的后验摘要方法捕捉多 plausible 符号模型的不确定性；(4)在有/无符号误设条件下建立后验集中性理论保证。

Result: 理论方面，在近似符号可实现性下给出了后验集中保证，且在精确符号可表示时达到近参数速率，同时在符号误设下建立了尖锐的Oracle集中性结果。实验方面，在基准科学方程学习上相比SOTA方法实现了更优的预测精度、最优符号复杂度和稳定结构恢复，在材料发现任务中识别出科学可解释的描述符。

Conclusion: 该概率框架以统一方式解决了符号回归中精度-复杂度平衡与不确定性量化两大核心挑战，并首次为该领域提供了系统的理论分析，在科学发现应用中具有显著潜力。

Abstract: Symbolic regression has emerged as a powerful tool for artificial intelligence-driven scientific discovery by learning interpretable analytical expressions that reveal governing relationships directly from data. Existing methods, however, often rely on heuristic search, struggle to balance predictive accuracy with expression complexity in noisy settings, and offer limited characterization of symbolic uncertainty. Probabilistic approaches that address these challenges in a unified manner remain underexplored. We introduce a probabilistic symbolic regression framework that represents mathematical expressions as ensembles of symbolic trees. A regularizing prior over tree topology controls expression complexity, while an Occam's window-based posterior summary captures uncertainty across multiple plausible symbolic models. Given the limited existing theoretical treatment of symbolic regression, we develop posterior concentration guarantees under approximate symbolic realizability, yielding a near-parametric rate for exact symbolic representability. Additionally, we establish a sharp oracle concentration result under symbolic misspecification. Comparisons of our proposed framework with state-of-the-art competitors demonstrate superior predictive accuracy, optimal symbolic complexity, and stable structural recovery when learning benchmark scientific equations, together with the identification of scientifically interpretable descriptors in a challenging materials discovery problem.

</details>


### [265] [A Functional Approach to Curve Alignment and Shape Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.05632)
*Issam-Ali Moindjié,Cédric Beaulac,Marie-Hélène Descary*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于函数型数据分析（FDA）的形状分析框架，通过基展开技术解析地估计缩放、平移、旋转和重参数化等形变变量以实现曲线对齐，并利用PCA构建随机轮廓的生成模型，在传统FDA方法失效的场景中成功识别形变参数并捕捉轮廓的潜在分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统计形状分析主要依赖离散观测，忽视了轮廓的连续性和内在几何结构，且忽略了形变变量之间的依赖关系及其对形状的影响，导致统计信息丢失和可解释性下降。因此，需要一种能够保持连续结构、同时建模形变变量的分析框架。

Method: 将形状分析置于函数型数据分析（FDA）框架下，利用基展开技术推导缩放、平移、旋转和重参数化等形变变量的解析解以实现曲线对齐；在此基础上，基于主成分分析（PCA）构建随机轮廓的生成模型。

Result: 在模拟数据和MPEG-7数据库上的数值实验表明，该方法能够成功识别形变参数，并在传统FDA方法失效的情况下有效捕捉随机轮廓的潜在分布。

Conclusion: 所提出的FDA形状分析框架通过连续建模和形变变量的解析估计，克服了传统离散方法的局限性，在形状对齐和分布建模方面展现出显著优势。

Abstract: In many image analysis problems, the contours of objects carry important statistical information about shape. Such contours are typically affected by deformation variables including scaling, translation, rotation, and reparametrization. Previous studies in statistical shape analysis have mainly focused on analyzing contours and shapes through discrete observations. While this approach might offer computational advantages, it overlooks the continuous nature of these objects and their underlying geometric structure. It also ignores potential dependencies between the deformation variables and their effect on the shape, which may result in a loss of statistical information and reduced interpretability. In this paper, we introduce a novel framework for analyzing shapes within the context of Functional Data Analysis (FDA). Basis expansion techniques are employed to derive analytic solutions for the estimation of deformation variables, namely scaling, translation, rotation, and reparametrization, thereby achieving curve alignment. A generative model for random contours is then developed using principal component analysis techniques.
